--!nocheck
--!nolint

local __ANTIBRAIN_MODULES = {cache = {}}

do
    do
        local function __modImpl()
            local Failure = {}

            Failure.traceback = function(problem)
                local ok, text = pcall(tostring, problem)

                return debug.traceback((ok and {text} or {
                    'An unprintable error was raised',
                })[1], 2)
            end
            Failure.capture = function(callback, ...)
                local ok, message = xpcall(callback, Failure.traceback, ...)

                return (ok and {nil} or {message})[1]
            end

            function Failure.summary(failure)
                return failure:split('\n')[1]
            end

            return table.freeze(Failure)
        end

        function __ANTIBRAIN_MODULES.a()
            local v = __ANTIBRAIN_MODULES.cache.a

            if not v then
                v = {
                    c = __modImpl(),
                }
                __ANTIBRAIN_MODULES.cache.a = v
            end

            return v.c
        end
    end
    do
        local function __modImpl()
            return table.freeze({})
        end

        function __ANTIBRAIN_MODULES.b()
            local v = __ANTIBRAIN_MODULES.cache.b

            if not v then
                v = {
                    c = __modImpl(),
                }
                __ANTIBRAIN_MODULES.cache.b = v
            end

            return v.c
        end
    end
    do
        local function __modImpl()
            local Failure = __ANTIBRAIN_MODULES.a()
            local REPORTS_PER_CONNECTION = 5
            local HIDDEN_NOTICE = 'Further errors from this connection are not reported.'
            local REPORTS_PER_SCOPE = 10
            local Class = {}

            Class.__index = Class

            local Scope = {}

            function Scope.new(report, label)
                local fields = {
                    closed = false,
                    label = label,
                    _report = report,
                    _tail = nil,
                    _reported = {},
                    _reports = 0,
                }

                return setmetatable(fields, Class)
            end

            local function fail(scope, failure)
                if not failure then
                    return
                end

                local text = (scope.label and {
                    string.format('[%s] %s', tostring(scope.label), tostring(failure)),
                } or {failure})[1]
                local report = scope._report

                if report then
                    pcall(report, text)
                else
                    warn(text)
                end
            end
            local function unlink(scope, entry)
                if not entry.attached then
                    return
                end

                entry.attached = false

                if entry.previous then
                    entry.previous.next = entry.next
                end
                if entry.next then
                    entry.next.previous = entry.previous
                else
                    scope._tail = entry.previous
                end

                entry.previous = nil
                entry.next = nil
            end
            local function dispose(scope, cleanup)
                fail(scope, Failure.capture(cleanup))
            end

            function Class.add(self, cleanup)
                if self.closed then
                    dispose(self, cleanup)

                    return function() end
                end

                local tail = self._tail
                local entry = {
                    cleanup = cleanup,
                    previous = tail,
                    next = nil,
                    attached = true,
                }

                if tail then
                    tail.next = entry
                end

                self._tail = entry

                return function()
                    unlink(self, entry)
                end
            end

            local function release(object)
                local kind = typeof(object)

                if kind == 'Instance' then
                    object:Destroy()
                elseif kind == 'RBXScriptConnection' then
                    object:Disconnect()
                elseif kind == 'table' and type(object.Destroy) == 'function' then
                    object:Destroy()
                else
                    object:Remove()
                end
            end

            function Scope.own(scope, object)
                scope:add(function()
                    release(object)
                end)

                return object
            end
            function Class.child(self, label)
                local child = Scope.new(self._report, label or self.label)
                local detach = self:add(function()
                    child:close()
                end)

                child:add(detach)

                return child
            end
            function Class.detached(self, label)
                return Scope.new(self._report, label or self.label)
            end
            function Class.report(self, failure)
                local line = failure:match('^[^\n]*') or failure

                if self._reported[line] or self._reports >= REPORTS_PER_SCOPE then
                    return
                end

                self._reported[line] = true
                self._reports = self._reports + 1

                fail(self, failure)
            end
            function Class.connect(self, signal, callback)
                local reported = {}
                local remaining = REPORTS_PER_CONNECTION
                local connection = signal:Connect(function(...)
                    if self.closed then
                        return
                    end

                    local failure = Failure.capture(callback, ...)

                    if not failure or remaining == 0 then
                        return
                    end

                    local line = Failure.summary(failure)

                    if reported[line] then
                        return
                    end

                    reported[line] = true
                    remaining = remaining - 1

                    fail(self, (remaining == 0 and {
                        string.format('%s\n%s', tostring(failure), tostring(HIDDEN_NOTICE)),
                    } or {failure})[1])
                end)

                self:add(function()
                    connection:Disconnect()
                end)

                return connection
            end

            local function launch(self, callback, args, schedule)
                local detach = function() end
                local worker = coroutine.create(function()
                    if self.closed then
                        detach()

                        return
                    end

                    local failure = Failure.capture(callback, table.unpack(args, 1, args.n))

                    detach()
                    fail(self, failure)
                end)

                if self.closed then
                    return worker
                end

                detach = self:add(function()
                    if coroutine.status(worker) == 'suspended' then
                        task.cancel(worker)
                    end
                end)

                local failure = Failure.capture(schedule, worker)

                if failure then
                    detach()
                    error(failure, 0)
                end

                return worker
            end

            function Class.spawn(self, callback, ...)
                return launch(self, callback, table.pack(...), function(worker)
                    task.spawn(worker)
                end)
            end
            function Class.defer(self, callback, ...)
                return launch(self, callback, table.pack(...), function(worker)
                    task.defer(worker)
                end)
            end
            function Class.delay(self, seconds, callback, ...)
                assert(seconds >= 0 and seconds < math.huge, 'Delay must be finite and nonnegative')

                return launch(self, callback, table.pack(...), function(worker)
                    task.delay(seconds, worker)
                end)
            end
            function Class.close(self)
                if self.closed then
                    return
                end

                self.closed = true

                local entry = self._tail

                while entry do
                    unlink(self, entry)
                    dispose(self, entry.cleanup)

                    entry = self._tail
                end
            end

            return table.freeze(Scope)
        end

        function __ANTIBRAIN_MODULES.c()
            local v = __ANTIBRAIN_MODULES.cache.c

            if not v then
                v = {
                    c = __modImpl(),
                }
                __ANTIBRAIN_MODULES.cache.c = v
            end

            return v.c
        end
    end
    do
    local function __modImpl()
        local Services = __ANTIBRAIN_MODULES.k()

        __ANTIBRAIN_MODULES.c()

        local MAX_ESP = 60

        local function makePool(parent)
            local gui = Instance.new('ScreenGui')
            gui.Name           = 'AntiBrain_ESP'
            gui.DisplayOrder   = 12
            gui.IgnoreGuiInset = true
            gui.ResetOnSpawn   = false
            gui.ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling
            gui.Parent         = parent

            local pool = {}

            local pool = {}

for index = 1, MAX_ESP do
    local box = Instance.new('Frame')
    box.Visible                = false
    box.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    box.BorderSizePixel        = 0
    box.ZIndex                 = 1

    local fill = Instance.new('Frame')
    fill.Visible                = false
    fill.Size                   = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
    fill.BackgroundColor3       = Color3.new(0, 0, 0)
    fill.BackgroundTransparency = 0.6
    fill.BorderSizePixel        = 0
    fill.ZIndex                 = 0
    fill.Parent                 = box

        local outline = Instance.new('UIStroke')
    outline.ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border
    outline.LineJoinMode    = Enum.LineJoinMode.Miter
    outline.Thickness       = 2
    outline.Color           = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
    outline.Enabled         = false
    outline.Parent          = box

                local nameLabel = Instance.new('TextLabel')
                nameLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                nameLabel.TextSize               = 15
                nameLabel.Font                   = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold
                nameLabel.TextColor3             = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
                nameLabel.Text                   = ''
                nameLabel.Parent                 = box

                local distLabel = Instance.new('TextLabel')
                distLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                distLabel.TextSize               = 18
                distLabel.Font                   = Enum.Font.SourceSans
                distLabel.TextColor3             = Color3.new(0.85, 0.85, 0.85)
                distLabel.Text                   = ''
                distLabel.Parent                 = box

                local hpBar = Instance.new('Frame')
                hpBar.Size             = UDim2.new(10, 0, 0, 9)
                hpBar.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0)
                hpBar.BorderSizePixel  = 0
                hpBar.Parent           = box

                local hpFill = Instance.new('Frame')
                hpFill.Size             = UDim2.new(-1, 0, -1, 0)
                hpFill.BorderSizePixel  = 0
                hpFill.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(0, 1, 0)
                hpFill.Parent           = hpBar

                box.Parent = gui

-- ▼▼▼ Step 4-A 新增：head dot + tracer
local headDot = Instance.new('Frame')
headDot.Name                = 'HeadDot'
headDot.Size                = UDim2.fromOffset(6, 6)
headDot.AnchorPoint         = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
headDot.BackgroundColor3    = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
headDot.BorderSizePixel     = 0
headDot.Visible             = false
headDot.ZIndex              = 2
headDot.Parent              = box
local headDotCorner = Instance.new('UICorner')
headDotCorner.CornerRadius  = UDim.new(1, 0)
headDotCorner.Parent        = headDot

local tracer = Instance.new('Frame')
tracer.Name                 = 'Tracer'
tracer.AnchorPoint          = Vector2.new(0, 0.5)
tracer.BackgroundColor3     = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
tracer.BorderSizePixel      = 0
tracer.Size                 = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 1)
tracer.Visible              = false
tracer.ZIndex               = 1
tracer.Parent               = gui    -- 挂在 gui 上，不是 box

-- ▼▼▼ corner box 的 8 条边角线段，直接挂 gui
local corners = {}
local CORNER_NAMES = {'tl_h','tl_v','tr_h','tr_v','bl_h','bl_v','br_h','br_v'}
for _, cname in CORNER_NAMES do
    local cf = Instance.new('Frame')
    cf.Name              = 'Corner_' .. cname
    cf.BackgroundColor3  = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
    cf.BorderSizePixel   = 0
    cf.Visible           = false
    cf.ZIndex            = 2
    cf.Parent            = gui
    corners[cname] = cf
end
-- ▲▲▲

-- ▼▼▼ ASBP-S1：新增视觉元素
local healthText = Instance.new('TextLabel')
healthText.BackgroundTransparency = 1
healthText.TextSize               = 13
healthText.Font                   = Enum.Font.SourceSans
healthText.TextColor3             = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
healthText.Text                   = ''
healthText.Visible                = false
healthText.TextXAlignment         = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
healthText.Parent                 = box

local weaponLabel = Instance.new('TextLabel')
weaponLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
weaponLabel.TextSize               = 13
weaponLabel.Font                   = Enum.Font.SourceSans
weaponLabel.TextColor3             = Color3.fromRGB(255, 220, 100)
weaponLabel.Text                   = ''
weaponLabel.Visible                = false
weaponLabel.TextXAlignment         = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
weaponLabel.Parent                 = box
-- ▲▲▲ ASBP-S1

pool[index] = {
    box = box, outline = outline, fill = fill,
    name = nameLabel, dist = distLabel,
    hpBar = hpBar, hpFill = hpFill,
    headDot = headDot,
    tracer  = tracer,
    corners = corners,
    healthText  = healthText,
    weaponLabel = weaponLabel,
}
-- ▲▲▲ Step 4-A 结束
            end

            return { gui = gui, pool = pool }
        end

                local function hideAll(esp)
    for index = 1, MAX_ESP do
        local cell = esp.pool[index]
        cell.box.Visible = false
        for _, cf in cell.corners do cf.Visible = false end
        if cell.healthText  then cell.healthText.Visible  = false end
        if cell.weaponLabel then cell.weaponLabel.Visible = false end
    end
end

        local function update(esp, settings)
            local options = settings()

            if not options.active then
                hideAll(esp)
                return
            end

            local player    = Services.Players.LocalPlayer
            local character = player.Character

            if not character then
                hideAll(esp); return
            end

            local myRoot = character:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart')
            if not myRoot then
                hideAll(esp); return
            end

            local camera = Services.Workspace.CurrentCamera
            if not camera then
                hideAll(esp); return
            end

            local teamCheck = options.teamCheck ~= false
local myTeam    = if teamCheck then player:GetAttribute('TeamID') else nil
local enemies   = {}

for _, other in Services.Players:GetPlayers() do
    if other ~= player and (not teamCheck or other:GetAttribute('TeamID') ~= myTeam) then
                    local otherChar = other.Character
                    if otherChar then
                        local root     = otherChar:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart')
                        local head     = otherChar:FindFirstChild('Head')
                        local humanoid = otherChar:FindFirstChildOfClass('Humanoid')
                        if root and head and humanoid and humanoid.Health > 0 then
                            table.insert(enemies, {
                                root     = root,
                                head     = head,
                                humanoid = humanoid,
                                player   = other,
                            })
                        end
                    end
                end
            end

        local used = 1
local maxDistance = options.maxDistance or math.huge
local textSize    = options.textSize or 15

for _, enemy in enemies do
    if used > MAX_ESP then break end

    local worldDistance = (myRoot.Position - enemy.root.Position).Magnitude
local headPos, onScreen = camera:WorldToViewportPoint(enemy.head.Position)

-- ▼▼▼ ASBP-RANGE-S2：maxDistance 拉到 0 视为无限
local inRange = (maxDistance <= 0) or (worldDistance <= maxDistance)
if onScreen and inRange then
-- ▲▲▲ ASBP-RANGE-S2
        local height = (enemy.root.Position - enemy.head.Position).Magnitude * 3.0
        local width  = height
        local boxX   = headPos.X - width / 2
        local boxY   = headPos.Y

        local healthRatio = enemy.humanoid.Health / math.max(enemy.humanoid.MaxHealth, 1)
        local autoColor   = Color3.fromHSV(healthRatio * 0.33, 1, 1)

        local cell = esp.pool[used]
        cell.box.Visible  = true
        cell.box.Position = UDim2.new(0, boxX, 0, boxY)
        cell.box.Size     = UDim2.new(0, width, 0, height)

                if options.box then
            local boxColor = options.boxColor or Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
            local boxStyle = options.boxStyle or 'full box'

            if boxStyle == 'full box' then
                -- UIStroke 全框
                cell.outline.Enabled = true
                cell.outline.Color   = boxColor
                -- 隐藏所有 corner
                for _, cf in cell.corners do cf.Visible = false end

            elseif boxStyle == 'corner box' then
                -- 关掉全框
                cell.outline.Enabled = false
                -- 计算 corner 尺寸
                local cLen = math.max(4, math.floor(width * 0.25))
                local t    = 2
                local x1, y1 = boxX,           boxY
                local x2, y2 = boxX + width,   boxY + height
                -- TL
                cell.corners.tl_h.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(x1 - 1, y1 - 1)
                cell.corners.tl_h.Size     = UDim2.fromOffset(cLen, t)
                cell.corners.tl_v.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(x1 - 1, y1 - 1)
                cell.corners.tl_v.Size     = UDim2.fromOffset(t, cLen)
                -- TR
                cell.corners.tr_h.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(x2 - cLen + 1, y1 - 1)
                cell.corners.tr_h.Size     = UDim2.fromOffset(cLen, t)
                cell.corners.tr_v.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(x2 - t + 1, y1 - 1)
                cell.corners.tr_v.Size     = UDim2.fromOffset(t, cLen)
                -- BL
                cell.corners.bl_h.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(x1 - 1, y2 - t + 1)
                cell.corners.bl_h.Size     = UDim2.fromOffset(cLen, t)
                cell.corners.bl_v.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(x1 - 1, y2 - cLen + 1)
                cell.corners.bl_v.Size     = UDim2.fromOffset(t, cLen)
                -- BR
                cell.corners.br_h.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(x2 - cLen + 1, y2 - t + 1)
                cell.corners.br_h.Size     = UDim2.fromOffset(cLen, t)
                cell.corners.br_v.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(x2 - t + 1, y2 - cLen + 1)
                cell.corners.br_v.Size     = UDim2.fromOffset(t, cLen)
                -- 统一着色 + 显示
                for _, cf in cell.corners do
                    cf.BackgroundColor3 = boxColor
                    cf.Visible          = true
                end
            end
        else
            cell.outline.Enabled = false
            for _, cf in cell.corners do cf.Visible = false end
        end

        if options.boxFill then
            cell.fill.Visible                = true
            cell.fill.BackgroundColor3       = options.boxFillColor or Color3.new(0, 0, 0)
            cell.fill.BackgroundTransparency = 0.6
        else
            cell.fill.Visible = false
        end

        if options.name then
            cell.name.Text       = enemy.player.Name
            cell.name.TextSize   = textSize
            cell.name.TextColor3 = options.nameColor or Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
            cell.name.Position   = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, -(textSize + 1))
            cell.name.Visible    = true
        else
            cell.name.Visible = false
        end

        if options.distance then
            cell.dist.Text       = math.floor(worldDistance) .. 'm'
            cell.dist.TextSize   = textSize + 2
            cell.dist.TextColor3 = options.distanceColor or Color3.fromRGB(216, 216, 216)
            cell.dist.Position   = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 1, textSize + 1)
            cell.dist.Visible    = true
        else
            cell.dist.Visible = false
        end

        if options.health then
    cell.hpBar.Visible = true
    cell.hpFill.Size   = UDim2.new(healthRatio, 1, 1, 0)
    if options.healthAuto ~= false then
        cell.hpFill.BackgroundColor3 = autoColor
    else
        cell.hpFill.BackgroundColor3 = options.healthColor or Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 0)
    end
else
    cell.hpBar.Visible = false
end

-- ▼▼▼ Step 4-B 新增：head dot + tracer 更新逻辑
if options.headDot then
    cell.headDot.Visible = true
    cell.headDot.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(headPos.X, headPos.Y)
    cell.headDot.BackgroundColor3 = options.headDotColor or Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
else
    cell.headDot.Visible = false
end

if options.tracer then
    local viewport = camera.ViewportSize
    local originX  = viewport.X / 2
    local originY  = viewport.Y
    local dx       = headPos.X - originX
    local dy       = headPos.Y - originY
    local length   = math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy)
    local angle    = math.deg(math.atan2(dy, dx))

    cell.tracer.Visible          = true
    cell.tracer.Position         = UDim2.fromOffset(originX, originY)
    cell.tracer.Size             = UDim2.fromOffset(length, 1)
    cell.tracer.Rotation         = angle
    cell.tracer.BackgroundColor3 = options.tracerColor or Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
else
    cell.tracer.Visible = false
end
-- ▲▲▲ Step 4-B 结束

-- ▼▼▼ ASBP-S3A：血量文字 + 武器名
if options.healthText then
    local hp    = math.floor(enemy.humanoid.Health)
    local maxHp = math.floor(math.max(enemy.humanoid.MaxHealth, 1))
    cell.healthText.Text       = string.format('%d / %d', hp, maxHp)
    cell.healthText.TextSize   = textSize
    cell.healthText.TextColor3 = options.healthTextColor or Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
    cell.healthText.Position   = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 1, textSize + 2)
    cell.healthText.Visible    = true
else
    cell.healthText.Visible = false
end

if options.weaponDisplay then
    local vmFolder = Services.Workspace:FindFirstChild('ViewModels')
    local wName    = ''
    if vmFolder then
        local pname = enemy.player.Name
        for _, child in vmFolder:GetChildren() do
            if string.sub(child.Name, 1, #pname) == pname then
                wName = string.sub(child.Name, #pname + 2)
                break
            end
        end
    end
    cell.weaponLabel.Text       = wName ~= '' and ('[' .. wName .. ']') or ''
    cell.weaponLabel.TextSize   = textSize
    cell.weaponLabel.TextColor3 = options.weaponColor or Color3.fromRGB(255, 220, 100)
    cell.weaponLabel.Position   = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, -(textSize * 2 + 4))
    cell.weaponLabel.Visible    = wName ~= ''
else
    cell.weaponLabel.Visible = false
end
-- ▲▲▲ ASBP-S3A

-- ▼▼▼ ASBP-S3B：可见检测 + 警报 + 框线粗细
if options.visCheck and options.box then
    local vcParams = RaycastParams.new()
    vcParams.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
    local excl = {}
    for _, p in Services.Players:GetPlayers() do
        if p.Character then table.insert(excl, p.Character) end
    end
    local vmf = Services.Workspace:FindFirstChild('ViewModels')
    if vmf then table.insert(excl, vmf) end
    vcParams.FilterDescendantsInstances = excl
    local rayHit = Services.Workspace:Raycast(
        camera.CFrame.Position,
        enemy.head.Position - camera.CFrame.Position,
        vcParams)
    if rayHit then
        local hcol = options.hiddenColor or Color3.fromRGB(255, 80, 80)
        if cell.outline.Enabled  then cell.outline.Color = hcol end
        for _, cf in cell.corners do
            if cf.Visible then cf.BackgroundColor3 = hcol end
        end
    end
end

if options.alertDist and options.alertDist > 0 and worldDistance <= options.alertDist then
    if math.floor(tick() * 8) % 2 == 0 then
        if cell.outline.Enabled then
            cell.outline.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 40, 40)
        end
    end
end

if options.boxThickness and options.boxThickness ~= 2 then
    cell.outline.Thickness = options.boxThickness
end
-- ▲▲▲ ASBP-S3B

used = used + 1
    end
end

            for index = used, MAX_ESP do
    local cell = esp.pool[index]
    cell.box.Visible     = false
        -- ▼▼▼ Step 4-C
        if cell.headDot then cell.headDot.Visible = false end
    if cell.tracer  then cell.tracer.Visible  = false end
    for _, cf in cell.corners do cf.Visible = false end
    if cell.healthText  then cell.healthText.Visible  = false end
    if cell.weaponLabel then cell.weaponLabel.Visible = false end
    -- ▲▲▲
end
        end

        local ESP = {}

        function ESP.start(run, settings)
            local parent = (type(gethui) == 'function' and gethui() or game:GetService('CoreGui'))
            local esp    = makePool(parent)

            run:add(function()
                esp.gui:Destroy()
            end)

            run:connect(Services.RunService.RenderStepped, function()
                local ok, err = pcall(update, esp, settings)
                if not ok then
                    run:report(string.format('ESP update failed: %s', tostring(err)))
                end
            end)
        end

        return table.freeze(ESP)
    end

    function __ANTIBRAIN_MODULES.bd()
        local v = __ANTIBRAIN_MODULES.cache.bd

        if not v then
            v = {
                c = __modImpl(),
            }
            __ANTIBRAIN_MODULES.cache.bd = v
        end

        return v.c
    end
end
do
    local function __modImpl()
        local Services = __ANTIBRAIN_MODULES.k()
        local Modules  = __ANTIBRAIN_MODULES.l()

        __ANTIBRAIN_MODULES.c()

        local player = Services.Players.LocalPlayer

        local function getHumanoid()
            local char = player.Character
            return char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass('Humanoid')
        end

        local Tools = {}

        function Tools.speed(run, getSpeed)
            local conn = nil
            local function apply()
                local hum = getHumanoid()
                if not hum then return end
                local v = getSpeed()
                hum.WalkSpeed = v
                if conn then conn:Disconnect() end
                conn = hum:GetPropertyChangedSignal('WalkSpeed'):Connect(function()
                    if not run.closed and hum.WalkSpeed ~= v then
                        hum.WalkSpeed = v
                    end
                end)
            end
            apply()
            run:connect(player.CharacterAdded, function()
                task.wait(0.5)
                if not run.closed then apply() end
            end)
            run:add(function()
                if conn then conn:Disconnect(); conn = nil end
                local hum = getHumanoid()
                if hum then hum.WalkSpeed = 16 end
            end)
        end

        function Tools.infiniteJump(run)
            run:connect(Services.UserInputService.JumpRequest, function()
                local hum = getHumanoid()
                if hum and hum.Health > 0 then
                    hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping)
                end
            end)
        end

        function Tools.slideBoost(run, getSpeed)
            local mech = nil
            local function load()
                local ok, res = pcall(function()
                    return require(player.PlayerScripts.Controllers.MechanicsController)
                end)
                mech = ok and res or nil
            end
            load()
            run:connect(player.CharacterAdded, function()
                task.wait(1)
                if not run.closed then load() end
            end)
            run:connect(Services.RunService.RenderStepped, function()
                if not mech then load() end
                if mech and mech.IsSliding then
                    pcall(function()
                        mech._sliding_velocity.Velocity =
                            mech._sliding_velocity.Velocity.Unit * getSpeed()
                    end)
                end
            end)
        end

        function Tools.deviceSpoof(run, getDevice)
            local remote = Services.ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild('Remotes')
            remote = remote and remote:FindFirstChild('Replication')
            remote = remote and remote:FindFirstChild('Fighter')
            remote = remote and remote:FindFirstChild('SetControls')
            if not remote then return end

            local ok, ctrl = pcall(function()
                return Modules.controller('FighterController')
            end)
            if not ok or type(ctrl) ~= 'table' then return end
            if type(ctrl._ReplicateControls) ~= 'function' then return end

            local original = ctrl._ReplicateControls
            ctrl._ReplicateControls = function(self)
                pcall(function() remote:FireServer(getDevice()) end)
                self._last_controls_replicated_time = tick()
            end
            pcall(function() remote:FireServer(getDevice()) end)
            run:add(function()
                ctrl._ReplicateControls = original
            end)
        end

        return table.freeze(Tools)
    end

    function __ANTIBRAIN_MODULES.be()
        local v = __ANTIBRAIN_MODULES.cache.be
        if not v then
            v = { c = __modImpl() }
            __ANTIBRAIN_MODULES.cache.be = v
        end
        return v.c
    end

do
    local function __modImpl()
        local Services = __ANTIBRAIN_MODULES.k()

        __ANTIBRAIN_MODULES.c()

        local player = Services.Players.LocalPlayer

        local function getGun()
            local ok, res = pcall(function()
                return require(player.PlayerScripts.Modules.ItemTypes.Gun)
            end)
            return ok and res or nil
        end

        local function getItemLibrary()
            local ok, res = pcall(function()
                return require(Services.ReplicatedStorage.Modules.ItemLibrary)
            end)
            return ok and res or nil
        end

        local originalRapidFire = nil
        local function captureRapidFire()
            if originalRapidFire then return true end
            local lib = getItemLibrary()
            if not lib or type(lib.Items) ~= 'table' then return false end
            local saved = {}
            for name, data in pairs(lib.Items) do
                if typeof(data) == 'table' then
                    local entry = {}
                    if data.ShootCooldown      ~= nil then entry.ShootCooldown      = data.ShootCooldown end
                    if data.ShootBurstCooldown ~= nil then entry.ShootBurstCooldown = data.ShootBurstCooldown end
                    if data.ShootSpread        ~= nil then entry.ShootSpread        = data.ShootSpread end
                    if data.ShootAccuracy      ~= nil then entry.ShootAccuracy      = data.ShootAccuracy end
                    if data.ShootRecoil        ~= nil then entry.ShootRecoil        = data.ShootRecoil end
                    if next(entry) then saved[name] = entry end
                end
            end
            originalRapidFire = saved
            return true
        end
        task.spawn(function()
            task.wait(2)
            pcall(captureRapidFire)
        end)

        local Tools = {}

        function Tools.rapidFire(run, getDelay)
            captureRapidFire()
            if not originalRapidFire then return end
            local lib = getItemLibrary()
            if not lib or type(lib.Items) ~= 'table' then return end
            local items = lib.Items
            local lastDelay = nil
            local function apply()
                local delaySec = (getDelay() or 1) / 1000
                if delaySec == lastDelay then return end
                lastDelay = delaySec
                for name, data in pairs(items) do
                    if typeof(data) == 'table' and originalRapidFire[name] then
                        data.ShootCooldown      = delaySec
                        data.ShootBurstCooldown = delaySec
                        data.ShootSpread        = 0
                        data.ShootAccuracy      = 0
                        data.ShootRecoil        = 0
                    end
                end
            end
            apply()
            run:connect(Services.RunService.Heartbeat, function()
                if not run.closed then apply() end
            end)
            run:add(function()
                for name, entry in pairs(originalRapidFire) do
                    local data = items[name]
                    if data then
                        for k, v in pairs(entry) do data[k] = v end
                    end
                end
                lastDelay = nil
            end)
        end

        function Tools.instantAds(run)
            local Gun = getGun()
            if not Gun or type(Gun.StartAiming) ~= 'function' then return end
            local origStart = Gun.StartAiming
            local origSpeed = Gun.GetAimSpeed
            Gun.StartAiming = function(self)
                self:SetReplicate('IsAiming', true)
                self.StopSprinting:Fire()
                self.ViewModel:SetAiming(true)
                self:SetReplicate('FOVOffset', self.Info.AimFOVOffset)
                if self.ViewModel.CurrentAimValue then self.ViewModel.CurrentAimValue = 1 end
                return true, 'StartAiming'
            end
            Gun.GetAimSpeed = function() return 999 end
            run:add(function()
                Gun.StartAiming = origStart
                Gun.GetAimSpeed = origSpeed
            end)
        end

        function Tools.noEquipAnim(run)
            local Gun = getGun()
            if not Gun or type(Gun.Equip) ~= 'function' then return end
            local orig = Gun.Equip
            Gun.Equip = function(self, ...)
                local r = {orig(self, ...)}
                if self.ViewModel then
                    pcall(function() self.ViewModel:StopAnimation('Equip') end)
                    pcall(function() self.ViewModel:StopAnimation('EquipEmpty') end)
                end
                return table.unpack(r)
            end
            run:add(function()
                Gun.Equip = orig
            end)
        end

        function Tools.noShootAnim(run)
            local Gun = getGun()
            if not Gun then return end
            local stops = {'Shoot','Fire','ShootBurst','Shoot_ADS','ShootHip','ShootEmpty','ShootADS','Burst'}
            local origShoot, origFinish, origBurst = Gun.StartShooting, Gun.FinishShooting, Gun.ShootBurst
            if type(origShoot) == 'function' then
                Gun.StartShooting = function(self, ...)
                    local vm = self.ViewModel
                    local savedPlay
                    if vm then savedPlay = vm.PlayAnimation; vm.PlayAnimation = function() end end
                    local r = {origShoot(self, ...)}
                    if vm and savedPlay then
                        vm.PlayAnimation = savedPlay
                        for _, name in ipairs(stops) do
                            pcall(function() vm:StopAnimation(name) end)
                        end
                    end
                    return table.unpack(r)
                end
            end
            if type(origFinish) == 'function' then
                Gun.FinishShooting = function(self, ...)
                    local vm = self.ViewModel
                    local savedPlay
                    if vm then savedPlay = vm.PlayAnimation; vm.PlayAnimation = function() end end
                    local r = {origFinish(self, ...)}
                    if vm and savedPlay then
                        vm.PlayAnimation = savedPlay
                        pcall(function() vm:StopAnimation('Shoot') end)
                        pcall(function() vm:StopAnimation('Fire') end)
                    end
                    return table.unpack(r)
                end
            end
            if type(origBurst) == 'function' then
                Gun.ShootBurst = function(self, ...)
                    local vm = self.ViewModel
                    local savedPlay
                    if vm then savedPlay = vm.PlayAnimation; vm.PlayAnimation = function() end end
                    local r = {origBurst(self, ...)}
                    if vm and savedPlay then
                        vm.PlayAnimation = savedPlay
                        for _, name in ipairs(stops) do
                            pcall(function() vm:StopAnimation(name) end)
                        end
                    end
                    return table.unpack(r)
                end
            end
            run:add(function()
                if type(origShoot)  == 'function' then Gun.StartShooting  = origShoot end
                if type(origFinish) == 'function' then Gun.FinishShooting = origFinish end
                if type(origBurst)  == 'function' then Gun.ShootBurst     = origBurst end
            end)
        end

        function Tools.noSpread(run)
            local Gun = getGun()
            local origGun
            if Gun and type(Gun.GetSpread) == 'function' then
                origGun = Gun.GetSpread
                Gun.GetSpread = function() return Vector2.new(0, 0) end
            end
            local GU, origGU
            local ok, res = pcall(function()
                return require(Services.ReplicatedStorage.Modules.GameplayUtility)
            end)
            if ok then GU = res end
            if GU and type(GU.GetSpread) == 'function' then
                origGU = GU.GetSpread
                GU.GetSpread = function() return CFrame.identity end
            end
            run:add(function()
                if Gun and origGun then Gun.GetSpread = origGun end
                if GU  and origGU  then GU.GetSpread  = origGU  end
            end)
        end

        function Tools.noSmoke(run)
            local SmokeScreen, SmokeCloud
            local ok1, r1 = pcall(function()
                return require(player.PlayerScripts.Modules.ClientReplicatedClasses.ClientFighter.FighterInterface.SmokeScreen)
            end)
            if ok1 then SmokeScreen = r1 end
            local ok2, r2 = pcall(function()
                return require(player.PlayerScripts.Modules.SmokeCloud)
            end)
            if ok2 then SmokeCloud = r2 end
            local orig1 = SmokeScreen and SmokeScreen.Update
            local orig2 = SmokeCloud  and SmokeCloud.Update
            if SmokeScreen and type(orig1) == 'function' then
                SmokeScreen.Update = function(self)
                    pcall(function()
                        if self._smoke_cloud_spring then self._smoke_cloud_spring.Target = 0 end
                        if self._smoke_cloud_cover  then self._smoke_cloud_cover.Transparency = 1 end
                        if self._smoke_cloud_dof    then self._smoke_cloud_dof.Parent = nil end
                    end)
                end
            end
            if SmokeCloud and type(orig2) == 'function' then
                SmokeCloud.Update = function(self)
                    pcall(function()
                        if self.Model then self.Model:Destroy() end
                    end)
                    return true
                end
            end
            run:add(function()
                if SmokeScreen and orig1 then SmokeScreen.Update = orig1 end
                if SmokeCloud  and orig2 then SmokeCloud.Update  = orig2 end
            end)
        end

        function Tools.noFlash(run)
            local Flashed
            local ok, res = pcall(function()
                return require(player.PlayerScripts.Modules.ClientReplicatedClasses.ClientFighter.FighterInterface.Flashed)
            end)
            if ok then Flashed = res end
            local orig = Flashed and Flashed.Flash
            if Flashed and type(orig) == 'function' then
                Flashed.Flash = function() end
            end
            run:add(function()
                if Flashed and orig then Flashed.Flash = orig end
            end)
        end

        return table.freeze(Tools)
    end

    function __ANTIBRAIN_MODULES.bf()
        local v = __ANTIBRAIN_MODULES.cache.bf
        if not v then
            v = { c = __modImpl() }
            __ANTIBRAIN_MODULES.cache.bf = v
        end
        return v.c

do
    local function __modImpl()
        local Services = __ANTIBRAIN_MODULES.k()

        __ANTIBRAIN_MODULES.c()

        local player = Services.Players.LocalPlayer

        local function getGun()
            local ok, res = pcall(function()
                return require(player.PlayerScripts.Modules.ItemTypes.Gun)
            end)
            return ok and res or nil
        end
        local function getUtility()
            local ok, res = pcall(function()
                return require(Services.ReplicatedStorage.Modules.Utility)
            end)
            return ok and res or nil
        end
        local function getItemLibrary()
            local ok, res = pcall(function()
                return require(Services.ReplicatedStorage.Modules.ItemLibrary)
            end)
            return ok and res or nil
        end

        local Tools = {}

        -- ── 1. Noclip ──────────────────────────────────────
        function Tools.noclip(run)
            run:connect(Services.RunService.Stepped, function()
                local char = player.Character
                if not char then return end
                for _, part in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
                    if part:IsA('BasePart') then part.CanCollide = false end
                end
            end)
        end

        -- ── 2. Fly ─────────────────────────────────────────
        function Tools.fly(run, getSpeed)
            local attachment, velocity, align
            local humanoid, root

            local function setup()
                local char = player.Character
                if not char then return end
                humanoid = char:FindFirstChildOfClass('Humanoid')
                root = char:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart')
                if not (humanoid and root) then return end
                if attachment then attachment:Destroy() end
                humanoid.PlatformStand = true
                attachment = Instance.new('Attachment', root)
                velocity = Instance.new('LinearVelocity', attachment)
                velocity.MaxForce = 9e9
                velocity.VectorVelocity = Vector3.zero
                velocity.Attachment0 = attachment
                align = Instance.new('AlignOrientation', attachment)
                align.MaxTorque = 9e9
                align.Responsiveness = 200
                align.Mode = Enum.OrientationAlignmentMode.OneAttachment
                align.Attachment0 = attachment
            end
            setup()
            run:connect(player.CharacterAdded, function()
                task.wait(0.1)
                if not run.closed then setup() end
            end)
            local controls
            pcall(function()
                local pm = require(player.PlayerScripts:WaitForChild('PlayerModule'))
                controls = pm:GetControls()
            end)
            run:connect(Services.RunService.RenderStepped, function()
                if not (root and velocity and align) then return end
                local cam = Services.Workspace.CurrentCamera
                if not cam or not controls then return end
                local mv = controls:GetMoveVector()
                local speed = getSpeed()
                if mv.Magnitude > 0 then
                    velocity.VectorVelocity = (cam.CFrame.LookVector * -mv.Z + cam.CFrame.RightVector * mv.X).Unit * speed
                else
                    velocity.VectorVelocity = Vector3.zero
                end
                align.CFrame = cam.CFrame
            end)
            run:add(function()
                if humanoid then humanoid.PlatformStand = false end
                if attachment then attachment:Destroy() end
            end)
        end

        -- ── 3. Scythe（镰刀冷却归零）──────────────────────
        function Tools.scythe(run)
            local lib = getItemLibrary()
            if not lib or type(lib.Items) ~= 'table' then return end
            local saved = {}
            for name, item in pairs(lib.Items) do
                if type(item) == 'table' and string.lower(item.Name or name) == 'scythe' then
                    if item.DashCooldown ~= nil then
                        table.insert(saved, {item, 'DashCooldown', item.DashCooldown})
                        item.DashCooldown = 0
                    elseif item.AbilityCooldown ~= nil then
                        table.insert(saved, {item, 'AbilityCooldown', item.AbilityCooldown})
                        item.AbilityCooldown = 0
                    end
                end
            end
            run:add(function()
                for _, e in ipairs(saved) do e[1][e[2]] = e[3] end
            end)
        end

        -- ── 4. Wallbang ────────────────────────────────────
        function Tools.wallbang(run)
            local Gun = getGun()
            local Utility = getUtility()
            if not (Gun and Utility) then return end
            local original = Gun.StartShooting
            local target = nil

            local function findTarget()
                local myChar = player.Character
                if not myChar then return nil end
                local myRoot = myChar:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart')
                if not myRoot then return nil end
                local closest, closestDist = nil, math.huge
                local myTeam = player:GetAttribute('TeamID')
                for _, p in ipairs(Services.Players:GetPlayers()) do
                    if p ~= player and p:GetAttribute('TeamID') ~= myTeam then
                        local c = p.Character
                        if c then
                            local r = c:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart')
                            local h = c:FindFirstChildWhichIsA('Humanoid')
                            if r and h and h.Health > 0 then
                                local d = (myRoot.Position - r.Position).Magnitude
                                if d < closestDist then closest, closestDist = p, d end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
                return closest
            end

            local heartbeat = Services.RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
                if not run.closed then target = findTarget() end
            end)
            run:add(function() heartbeat:Disconnect() end)

            Gun.StartShooting = function(self, ...)
                if run.closed or not self.ClientFighter or not self.ClientFighter.IsLocalPlayer then
                    return original(self, ...)
                end
                local results = {original(self, ...)}
                local data = results[3]
                if not data or typeof(data) ~= 'table' then return table.unpack(results) end
                results[4] = true
                local t = target
                if not t or not t.Character then return table.unpack(results) end
                local head = t.Character:FindFirstChild('Head')
                if not head then return table.unpack(results) end
                local headPos = head.Position
                local below = headPos - Vector3.new(0, 5, 0)
                local cf = CFrame.lookAt(below, headPos)
                pcall(function()
                    data[utf8.char(0)] = Utility:EncodeCFrame(CFrame.new(below, headPos) * CFrame.Angles(cf:ToOrientation()))
                    data[utf8.char(1)] = Utility:EncodeCFrame(CFrame.new(headPos) * CFrame.Angles(cf:ToOrientation()))
                    data[utf8.char(2)] = head
                    data[utf8.char(3)] = Utility:EncodeCFrame(CFrame.new())
                end)
                return table.unpack(results)
            end
            run:add(function() Gun.StartShooting = original end)
        end

        -- ── 5. Wallbang Head ──────────────────────────────
        function Tools.wallbangHead(run)
            local Gun = getGun()
            local Utility = getUtility()
            if not (Gun and Utility) then return end
            local original = Gun.StartShooting
            local target = nil

            local function findTarget()
                local myChar = player.Character
                if not myChar then return nil end
                local myRoot = myChar:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart')
                if not myRoot then return nil end
                local closest, closestDist = nil, math.huge
                local myTeam = player:GetAttribute('TeamID')
                for _, p in ipairs(Services.Players:GetPlayers()) do
                    if p ~= player and p:GetAttribute('TeamID') ~= myTeam then
                        local c = p.Character
                        if c then
                            local r = c:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart')
                            local h = c:FindFirstChildWhichIsA('Humanoid')
                            if r and h and h.Health > 0 then
                                local d = (myRoot.Position - r.Position).Magnitude
                                if d < closestDist then closest, closestDist = p, d end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
                return closest
            end

            local heartbeat = Services.RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
                if not run.closed then target = findTarget() end
            end)
            run:add(function() heartbeat:Disconnect() end)

            Gun.StartShooting = function(self, ...)
                if run.closed or not self.ClientFighter or not self.ClientFighter.IsLocalPlayer then
                    return original(self, ...)
                end
                local results = {original(self, ...)}
                local data = results[3]
                if not data or typeof(data) ~= 'table' then return table.unpack(results) end
                results[4] = true
                local t = target
                if not t or not t.Character then return table.unpack(results) end
                local head = t.Character:FindFirstChild('Head')
                if not head then return table.unpack(results) end
                local headPos = head.Position
                local headCF = head.CFrame
                local offset = headCF:ToObjectSpace(CFrame.new(headPos + Vector3.new(math.random()*0.1, math.random()*0.1, math.random()*0.1)))
                pcall(function()
                    data[utf8.char(0)] = Utility:EncodeCFrame(CFrame.new(headPos, headPos + headCF.LookVector))
                    data[utf8.char(1)] = Utility:EncodeCFrame(CFrame.new(headPos))
                    data[utf8.char(2)] = head
                    data[utf8.char(3)] = Utility:EncodeCFrame(offset)
                end)
                return table.unpack(results)
            end
            run:add(function() Gun.StartShooting = original end)
        end

        return table.freeze(Tools)
    end

    function __ANTIBRAIN_MODULES.bg()
        local v = __ANTIBRAIN_MODULES.cache.bg
        if not v then
            v = { c = __modImpl() }
            __ANTIBRAIN_MODULES.cache.bg = v
        end
        return v.c
    end
end
-- ▼▼▼ OrbiterSystem bC
do
    local function __OrbiterSystemImpl(Services)
        local OrbiterSystem = {}
        local random = Random.new()

        local function getNearestEnemy(opponents)
            local best, bestDist = nil, math.huge
            for _, opp in opponents do
                if opp.health > 0 and opp.inside then
                    if opp.distance < bestDist then
                        bestDist = opp.distance
                        best = opp
                    end
                end
            end
            return best
        end

        function OrbiterSystem.step(state, deps, settings)
            if not settings.active then
                state.active = false
                return nil
            end

            local fighter = deps.fighter()
            local root    = fighter and deps.root()

            if not fighter or not root then
                state.active = false
                return 'scatter: no character'
            end

            local opponents = deps.opponents(root)
            local target    = getNearestEnemy(opponents)

            if not target then
                state.active = false
                return 'scatter: no target'
            end

            local ox    = settings.offsetX
            local oy    = settings.offsetY
            local burst = math.max(1, math.floor(settings.burstCount))
            local ep    = target.position

            local finalRot = root.CFrame.Rotation

            for _ = 1, burst do
                local rx = random:NextNumber(-ox, ox)
                local rz = random:NextNumber(-ox, ox)
                local ry = random:NextNumber(-oy, oy)

                root.CFrame = CFrame.new(
                    ep.x + rx,
                    ep.y + ry,
                    ep.z + rz
                ) * finalRot

                root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
            end

            state.active = true
            return string.format(
                'scatter: %s | x=%.1f y=%.1f burst=%d',
                tostring(target.name), ox, oy, burst
            )
        end

        function OrbiterSystem.loop(run, settings, deps, report)
            local state = { active = false }

            run:connect(Services.RunService.RenderStepped, function()
                local ok, result = pcall(OrbiterSystem.step, state, deps, settings())
                if report then
                    report(ok and result or ('scatter error: ' .. tostring(result)))
                end
            end)
        end

        return table.freeze(OrbiterSystem)
    end

    do
        local function __modImpl()
            local Services = __ANTIBRAIN_MODULES.k()
            __ANTIBRAIN_MODULES.c()
            return __OrbiterSystemImpl(Services)
        end

        function __ANTIBRAIN_MODULES.bC()
            local v = __ANTIBRAIN_MODULES.cache.bC
            if not v then
                v = { c = __modImpl() }
                __ANTIBRAIN_MODULES.cache.bC = v
            end
            return v.c
        end
    end
end
-- ▲▲▲ OrbiterSystem bC
-- ▼▼▼ OrbiterControls bD
do
    local function __modImpl()
        local OrbiterSystem = __ANTIBRAIN_MODULES.bC()
        local CounterSystem = __ANTIBRAIN_MODULES.K()
        local RageReport    = __ANTIBRAIN_MODULES.n()

        __ANTIBRAIN_MODULES.c()
        __ANTIBRAIN_MODULES.g()

        local OrbiterControls = {}

        function OrbiterControls.build(box, ui)
            local toggle = box:toggle(ui.id('orbiter'), {
                text    = 'scatter teleport',
                default = false,
                tooltip = [[每帧高频随机瞬移，绑 RenderStepped；burst 控制同帧写入次数]],
            })
            local key = toggle:keybind(ui.id('orbiter-key'), {
                text = 'scatter teleport',
            })
            local opts = box:dependent(function()
                return toggle.value
            end)
            local sliderX = opts:slider(ui.id('orbiter-x'), {
                text    = 'scatter X/Z',
                min     = 1, max = 50, default = 8, suffix = ' st',
                tooltip = [[X 和 Z 方向的最大随机偏移（studs）]],
            })
            local sliderY = opts:slider(ui.id('orbiter-y'), {
                text    = 'scatter Y',
                min     = 0, max = 20, default = 3, suffix = ' st',
                tooltip = [[高度方向的最大随机偏移（studs）]],
            })
            local sliderBurst = opts:slider(ui.id('orbiter-burst'), {
                text    = 'burst count',
                min     = 1, max = 8, default = 4, suffix = 'x',
                tooltip = [[同一帧内写入 CFrame 的次数；越高服务端越难锁定你的位置]],
            })
            local settings = {
                active     = false,
                offsetX    = 8,
                offsetY    = 3,
                burstCount = 4,
            }
            function OrbiterControls.start(run, services)
                local shown = false
                run:add(function()
                    if shown then RageReport.set(services, nil) end
                end)
                OrbiterSystem.loop(
                    run,
                    function()
                        settings.active     = key:active()
                        settings.offsetX    = sliderX.value
                        settings.offsetY    = sliderY.value
                        settings.burstCount = sliderBurst.value
                        return settings
                    end,
                    CounterSystem.resolve(run, services),
                    function(status)
                        if status ~= nil then
                            shown = true
                            RageReport.set(services, { status = status, target = nil })
                        elseif shown then
                            shown = false
                            RageReport.set(services, nil)
                        end
                    end
                )
            end
            return {
                toggle   = toggle,
                settings = function()
                    settings.active     = key:active()
                    settings.offsetX    = sliderX.value
                    settings.offsetY    = sliderY.value
                    settings.burstCount = sliderBurst.value
                    return settings
                end,
            }
        end

        return table.freeze(OrbiterControls)
    end

    function __ANTIBRAIN_MODULES.bD()
        local v = __ANTIBRAIN_MODULES.cache.bD
        if not v then
            v = { c = __modImpl() }
            __ANTIBRAIN_MODULES.cache.bD = v
        end
        return v.c
    end
end
-- ▲▲▲ OrbiterControls bD
do
    local function __modImpl()
        local Services = __ANTIBRAIN_MODULES.k()

        __ANTIBRAIN_MODULES.c()

        local player = Services.Players.LocalPlayer

        local function getRoot()
            local char = player.Character
            return char and char:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart')
        end

        local State = {
            framecounter = 0,
            smoothyaw    = 0,
            smoothpitch  = 0,
            smoothroll   = 0,
        }

        local Tools = {}

        local function getrandom(min, max)
            return min + math.random() * (max - min)
        end

        local function calculateYaw(s)
            local yaw = 0
            local t = tick()
            if s.yawtype == 'jitter' then
                local mn = math.rad(s.minangle)
                local mx = math.rad(s.maxangle)
                if s.randomangle then
                    yaw = getrandom(-mx, mx)
                else
                    yaw = math.random() > 0.5 and mn or -mn
                end
            elseif s.yawtype == 'spinbot' then
                local speed = getrandom(s.minspeed / 10, s.maxspeed / 10)
                yaw = (t * speed) % (2 * math.pi)
            elseif s.yawtype == 'random' then
                if State.framecounter % 30 == 0 then
                    yaw = getrandom(-math.rad(s.maxangle), math.rad(s.maxangle))
                else
                    yaw = State.smoothyaw
                end
            end
            State.smoothyaw = yaw
            return yaw
        end

        local function calculatePitch(s)
            local pitch = 0
            if s.pitchtype == 'jitter' then
                local mn = math.rad(s.minangle)
                local mx = math.rad(s.maxangle)
                if s.randomangle then
                    pitch = getrandom(-mx, mx)
                else
                    pitch = math.random() > 0.5 and mn or -mn
                end
            elseif s.pitchtype == 'spinbot' then
                pitch = math.sin(tick() * (s.maxspeed / 10)) * math.rad(s.maxangle)
            elseif s.pitchtype == 'random' then
                if State.framecounter % 20 == 0 then
                    pitch = getrandom(math.rad(-89), math.rad(89))
                else
                    pitch = State.smoothpitch
                end
            end
            State.smoothpitch = pitch
            return pitch
        end

        local function calculateRoll(s)
            local roll = 0
            if s.angletype == 'tilt 45' then
                roll = math.rad(45)
            elseif s.angletype == 'tilt 90' then
                roll = math.rad(90)
            elseif s.angletype == 'upside down' then
                roll = math.rad(180)
            elseif s.angletype == 'custom' then
                roll = math.rad(s.customangle)
            end
            State.smoothroll = roll
            return roll
        end

        function Tools.antiAim(run, getSettings)
            run:connect(Services.RunService.Heartbeat, function()
                local s = getSettings()
                if not s.enabled then return end
                if s.yawtype == 'none' and s.pitchtype == 'none' and s.angletype == 'none' then return end
                local root = getRoot()
                if not root then return end
                State.framecounter = State.framecounter + 1
                local y = calculateYaw(s)
                local p = calculatePitch(s)
                local r = calculateRoll(s)
                root.CFrame = root.CFrame * CFrame.Angles(p, y, r)
            end)
            run:connect(Services.RunService.Stepped, function()
                local s = getSettings()
                if not s.irregularMove then return end
                local root = getRoot()
                if not root then return end
                if root.Velocity.Magnitude > 1 then
                    local rx = math.random(-3, 3) / 10
                    local rz = math.random(-3, 3) / 10
                    root.CFrame = root.CFrame * CFrame.new(rx, 0, rz)
                end
            end)
            run:add(function()
                State.framecounter = 0
                State.smoothyaw    = 0
                State.smoothpitch  = 0
                State.smoothroll   = 0
            end)
        end

        return table.freeze(Tools)
    end

    function __ANTIBRAIN_MODULES.bh()
        local v = __ANTIBRAIN_MODULES.cache.bh
        if not v then
            v = { c = __modImpl() }
            __ANTIBRAIN_MODULES.cache.bh = v
        end
        return v.c
    end

do
    local function __modImpl()
        local Services = __ANTIBRAIN_MODULES.k()

        __ANTIBRAIN_MODULES.c()

        local GUI_PATH = {
            "PlayerGui", "MainGui", "PlayerList", "Container",
            "Elements", "Container", "Middle", "List", "Container",
        }

        local Spoof = {}

        function Spoof.start(run, getConfig)
            local Players = Services.Players
            local player = Players.LocalPlayer

            local function cfg()
                local c = getConfig()
                return c or {}
            end

            local function getListContainer()
                local node = player
                for _, name in ipairs(GUI_PATH) do
                    if not node then return nil end
                    node = node:FindFirstChild(name)
                end
                return node
            end

            local function findAllTitleLabels(instance, results)
                results = results or {}
                if not instance then return results end
                for _, child in ipairs(instance:GetChildren()) do
                    if child:IsA("TextLabel") and child.Name == "Title" then
                        table.insert(results, child)
                    end
                    findAllTitleLabels(child, results)
                end
                return results
            end

            local function spoofPlayerName(p, c)
                if not c.nameSpoof then return end
                local newName = (p == player) and c.yourName or c.enemyName
                if type(newName) ~= 'string' or newName == '' then return end
                pcall(function()
                    p.Name = newName
                    p.DisplayName = newName
                end)
            end

            local function spoofLeaderstats(c)
                if not c.levelSpoof and not c.winSpoof then return end
                local ls = player:FindFirstChild("CustomLeaderstats")
                if not ls then return end
                if c.levelSpoof then
                    local lv = ls:FindFirstChild("Level")
                    if lv and lv:IsA("IntValue") then
                        lv.Value = c.levelValue
                    end
                    pcall(function() player:SetAttribute("Level", c.levelValue) end)
                end
                if c.winSpoof then
                    local ws = ls:FindFirstChild("Win Streak")
                    if ws and ws:IsA("Folder") then
                        local sv = ws:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("IntValue")
                        if sv then sv.Value = c.winValue end
                    elseif ws and ws:IsA("IntValue") then
                        ws.Value = c.winValue
                    end
                    pcall(function() player:SetAttribute("StatisticDuelsWinStreak", c.winValue) end)
                end
            end

            local function spoofTitleLabels(container, c)
                if not container or not c.nameSpoof then return end
                for _, pf in ipairs(container:GetChildren()) do
                    if pf:IsA("Frame") then
                        local spoofed = false
                        local inner = pf:FindFirstChild("Container")
                        if inner and inner:IsA("Frame") then
                            for _, ch in ipairs(inner:GetChildren()) do
                                if ch:IsA("Frame") then
                                    local tl = ch:FindFirstChild("Title")
                                    if tl and tl:IsA("TextLabel") then
                                        local isLocal = tl.Text == player.Name or tl.Text == player.DisplayName
                                        local newName = isLocal and c.yourName or c.enemyName
                                        if type(newName) == 'string' and newName ~= '' then
                                            tl.Text = newName
                                        end
                                        spoofed = true
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                        end
                        if not spoofed then
                            local labels = findAllTitleLabels(pf)
                            for _, tl in ipairs(labels) do
                                local isLocal = tl.Text == player.Name or tl.Text == player.DisplayName
                                local newName = isLocal and c.yourName or c.enemyName
                                if type(newName) == 'string' and newName ~= '' then
                                    tl.Text = newName
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end

            -- 首次遍历已有玩家
            for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
                spoofPlayerName(p, cfg())
            end

            -- 新玩家加入
            run:connect(Players.PlayerAdded, function(p)
                if run.closed then return end
                spoofPlayerName(p, cfg())
            end)

            -- 监听 LocalPlayer 的 CustomLeaderstats 出现
            spoofLeaderstats(cfg())
            run:connect(player.ChildAdded, function(child)
                if run.closed then return end
                if child.Name == "CustomLeaderstats" then
                    task.wait(0.1)
                    spoofLeaderstats(cfg())
                end
            end)

            -- 每 3 秒轮询：刷新 leaderstats + 重新应用所有玩家名
            run:spawn(function()
                while not run.closed do
                    task.wait(3)
                    if run.closed then break end
                    local c = cfg()
                    if c.levelSpoof or c.winSpoof then
                        spoofLeaderstats(c)
                    end
                    if c.nameSpoof then
                        for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
                            spoofPlayerName(p, c)
                        end
                    end
                end
            end)

            -- GUI 循环：每 0.5 秒处理玩家列表 Title 文本
            run:spawn(function()
                local monitored = nil
                while not run.closed do
                    task.wait(0.5)
                    if run.closed then break end
                    local c = cfg()
                    if c.nameSpoof then
                        local lc = getListContainer()
                        if lc and lc ~= monitored then
                            monitored = lc
                            run:connect(lc.ChildAdded, function(child)
                                if run.closed then return end
                                if child:IsA("Frame") then
                                    task.wait(0.1)
                                    pcall(spoofTitleLabels, lc, cfg())
                                end
                            end)
                        end
                        if lc then
                            pcall(spoofTitleLabels, lc, c)
                        end
                    end
                end
            end)
        end

        return table.freeze(Spoof)
    end

    function __ANTIBRAIN_MODULES.bi()
        local v = __ANTIBRAIN_MODULES.cache.bi
        if not v then
            v = { c = __modImpl() }
            __ANTIBRAIN_MODULES.cache.bi = v
        end
        return v.c
    end

do
    local function __modImpl()
        __ANTIBRAIN_MODULES.g()

        local SpoofTab = {}

        function SpoofTab.build(ui)

    function __ANTIBRAIN_MODULES.bj()
        local v = __ANTIBRAIN_MODULES.cache.bj

        if not v then
            v = {
                c = __modImpl(),
            }
            __ANTIBRAIN_MODULES.cache.bj = v
