local box       = rightTabs:tab('indicators')
local espBox    = rightTabs:tab('esp')
                local function color(toggle, id, title)
                    return toggle:color(ui.id(id), {
                        title = title,
                        default = Color3.new(1, 1, 1),
                    })
                end

                local manipulated = box:toggle(ui.id('indicator-manipulated'), {
                    text = 'manipulated',
                    default = true,
                    tooltip = 'if your target is manipulated, this will show',
                })
                local manipulatedColor = color(manipulated, 'indicator-manipulated-color', 'manipulated color')
                local ragebot = box:toggle(ui.id('indicator-ragebot'), {
                    text = 'ragebot',
                    default = true,
                    tooltip = 'if ragebot is enabled, this will show',
                })
                local ragebotText = color(ragebot, 'indicator-ragebot-text-color', 'ragebot text color')
                local ragebotStatus = color(ragebot, 'indicator-ragebot-status-color', 'ragebot status color')
                local ragebotStyle = box:dependent(function()
                    return ragebot.value
                end):multiDropdown(ui.id('indicator-ragebot-style'), {
                    text = 'ragebot style',
                    values = {
                        'text',
                        'status',
                    },
                    default = {
                        'status',
                        'text',
                    },
                })
                local ammo = box:toggle(ui.id('indicator-ammo'), {
                    text = 'ammo',
                    default = false,
                })
                local ammoColor = color(ammo, 'indicator-ammo-color', 'ammo color')
                local dividerColor = color(ammo, 'indicator-ammo-divider-color', 'divider color')
                local maxAmmoColor = color(ammo, 'indicator-ammo-max-color', 'max ammo color')
                local reserveColor = color(ammo, 'indicator-ammo-reserve-color', 'reserve color')
                local emptyColor = color(ammo, 'indicator-ammo-empty-color', 'empty color')
                local ammoStyle = box:dependent(function()
                    return ammo.value
                end):multiDropdown(ui.id('indicator-ammo-style'), {
                    text = 'ammo style',
                    values = {
                        'ammo',
                        'max ammo',
                        'reserve',
                        Indicators.EMPTY,
                    },
                    default = {
                        'ammo',
                        'reserve',
                    },
                })

                box:divider()

                local lerp = box:slider(ui.id('indicator-lerp'), {
                    text = 'lerp',
                    default = 1,
                    min = 0.01,
                    max = 1,
                    rounding = 2,
                    suffix = 'x',
                })
                local offsetX = box:slider(ui.id('indicator-offset-x'), {
                    text = 'offset x',
                    default = 0,
                    min = -100,
                    max = 100,
                    rounding = 0,
                    increment = 5,
                    suffix = 'px',
                    tooltip = 'start offset x',
                })
                local offsetY = offsetX:slider(ui.id('indicator-offset-y'), {
                    text = 'offset y',
                    default = 25,
                    min = -100,
                    max = 100,
                    rounding = 0,
                    increment = 5,
                    suffix = 'px',
                    tooltip = 'start offset y',
                })
                local style = box:dropdown(ui.id('indicator-text-style'), {
                    text = 'text style',
                    values = {
                        'lower',
                        'upper',
                    },
                    default = 'lower',
                })
                local anchors = box:multiDropdown(ui.id('indicator-position'), {
                    text = 'position',
                    values = table.clone(FovControls.ANCHORS),
                    allowNull = true,
                })
                local white = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
                local settings = {
                    manipulated = true,
                    manipulatedColor = white,
                    ragebot = true,
                    ragebotStyle = {},
                    ragebotTextColor = white,
                    ragebotStatusColor = white,
                    ammo = false,
                    ammoStyle = {},
                    ammoColor = white,
                    dividerColor = white,
                    maxAmmoColor = white,
                    reserveColor = white,
                    emptyColor = white,
                    style = 'lower',
                    lerp = 1,
                    offset = Vector2.zero,
                    anchors = {},
                }

            boxColor      = espBoxColor.value,
            boxFill       = espBoxFill.value,
            boxFillColor  = espBoxFillColor.value,
            name          = espShowName.value,
            nameColor     = espNameColor.value,
            distance      = espShowDistance.value,
            distanceColor = espDistanceColor.value,
            health        = espShowHealth.value,
            healthAuto    = espHealthAuto.value,
            healthColor   = espHealthColor.value,
            textSize      = espTextSize.value,
maxDistance   = espMaxDistance.value,
teamCheck     = espTeamCheck.value,
    -- ▼▼▼ Step 5-B 新增
    headDot       = espShowHeadDot.value,
    headDotColor  = espHeadDotColor.value,
                tracer        = espShowTracer.value,
                tracerColor   = espTracerColor.value,
            boxStyle      = espBoxStyle.value,
-- ▼▼▼ ASBP-S5B：新返回键值
            healthText      = espHealthText.value,
            healthTextColor = espHealthTextColor.value,
            weaponDisplay   = espWeapon.value,
            weaponColor     = espWeaponColor.value,
            visCheck        = espVisCheck.value,
            hiddenColor     = espHiddenColor.value,
            boxThickness    = espBoxThickness.value,
            alertDist       = espAlertDist.value,
-- ▲▲▲ ASBP-S5B
}
    end,
}
end

                    return table.freeze(Visuals)
    end

    function __ANTIBRAIN_MODULES.ao()
        local v = __ANTIBRAIN_MODULES.cache.ao

        if not v then
            v = {
                c = __modImpl(),
            }
            __ANTIBRAIN_MODULES.cache.ao = v
        end

        return v.c
    end
end

-- ============================================================
-- ▼▼▼ Step 1 新增：Melee 配置 + HvH Beat UE 引擎
-- ============================================================
do
    local function __modImpl()
        local Services = __ANTIBRAIN_MODULES.k()
        local Modules  = __ANTIBRAIN_MODULES.l()

        __ANTIBRAIN_MODULES.c()

        local player = Services.Players.LocalPlayer

        -- ── Melee 状态 ─────────────────────────────────
        local MeleeCfg = {
            MeleeMinDelay = 0.105,
        }

        -- ── HvH 状态 ──────────────────────────────────
        local HvhCfg = {
            Enabled     = false,
            FireRate    = 0.00015,
            WeaponSlot  = 'Primary',
            FollowSpeed = 1000,
        }

                -- ▼▼▼ FakePos 新增：状态块 + 辅助函数
        local _fpPlayers = Services.Players
        local _fpRS      = Services.RunService

        local _fpState = {
            Enabled           = false,
            Depth             = 5.8,
            WalkMode          = true,
            SurfaceCamera     = true,
            AutoNoclip        = true,
            VisualizerEnabled = true,
            VisualizerColor   = Color3.fromRGB(0, 162, 255),
            Panic             = false,
            PanicMinX         = -5,
            PanicMaxX         = 5,
            PanicZ            = false,
            HideMeshes        = true,
            AnimationDesync   = true,
            ZeroVelocity      = true,
            CustomPitch       = 0,
            CustomYaw         = 0,
            InvertPitch       = false,
            VirtualCF         = nil,
            GhostBox          = nil,
            AnimTrack         = nil,
            OriginalParts     = nil,
            HandoffTicks      = 0,
            ClientCF          = nil,
            FakeCF            = nil,
        }

        local function _fpCaptureOriginal()
            local char = _fpPlayers.LocalPlayer and _fpPlayers.LocalPlayer.Character
            if not char then return end
            _fpState.OriginalParts = {}
            for _, part in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
                if part:IsA("BasePart") or part:IsA("Decal") then
                    table.insert(_fpState.OriginalParts, { part = part, transparency = part.Transparency })
                    if _fpState.HideMeshes then pcall(function() part.Transparency = 1 end) end
                end
            end
        end

        local function _fpRestoreOriginal()
            if not _fpState.OriginalParts then return end
            for _, data in ipairs(_fpState.OriginalParts) do
                if data.part and data.part.Parent then
                    pcall(function() data.part.Transparency = data.transparency end)
                end
            end
            _fpState.OriginalParts = nil
        end

        local function _fpPlayAnimDesync()
            local char = _fpPlayers.LocalPlayer and _fpPlayers.LocalPlayer.Character
            local hum  = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if not hum then return end
            if not _fpState.AnimTrack then
                pcall(function()
                    local anim = Instance.new("Animation")
                    anim.AnimationId = "rbxassetid://10714003221"
                    local track = hum:LoadAnimation(anim)
                    track:Play(0, 1, 0)
                    track.TimePosition = 1.48
                    _fpState.AnimTrack = track
                end)
            end
        end

        local function _fpStopAnimDesync()
            if _fpState.AnimTrack then
                pcall(function() _fpState.AnimTrack:Stop(); _fpState.AnimTrack:Destroy() end)
                _fpState.AnimTrack = nil
            end
        end

        local function _fpUpdateGhostVisualizer()
            if _fpState.Enabled and _fpState.VisualizerEnabled and _fpState.VirtualCF then
                if not _fpState.GhostBox or not _fpState.GhostBox.Parent then
                    local box = Instance.new("BoxHandleAdornment")
                    box.Name         = "_GravFakePosGhost"
                    box.Size         = Vector3.new(2, 5, 1)
                    box.Color3       = _fpState.VisualizerColor
                    box.Transparency = 0.55
                    box.AlwaysOnTop  = true
                    box.ZIndex       = 5
                    box.Adornee      = workspace.Terrain
                    pcall(function() box.Parent = game:GetService("CoreGui") end)
                    _fpState.GhostBox = box
                end
                _fpState.GhostBox.CFrame = _fpState.VirtualCF
                _fpState.GhostBox.Color3 = _fpState.VisualizerColor
            else
                if _fpState.GhostBox then
                    pcall(function() _fpState.GhostBox:Destroy() end)
                    _fpState.GhostBox = nil
                end
            end
        end

        local function _fpSetDesync(source, clientCF, serverCF)
            local genv = getgenv()
            genv.position_desync_source = source
            genv.client_position = typeof(clientCF) == "CFrame" and clientCF.Position or clientCF
            genv.server_position = typeof(serverCF) == "CFrame" and serverCF.Position or serverCF
            _fpState.ClientCF = clientCF
            _fpState.FakeCF   = serverCF
        end

        local function _fpClearDesync()
            local genv = getgenv()
            local char = _fpPlayers.LocalPlayer and _fpPlayers.LocalPlayer.Character
            local hrp  = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            local pos  = hrp and hrp.Position
            genv.position_desync_source = nil
            genv.client_position = pos
            genv.server_position = pos
            _fpState.ClientCF = nil
            _fpState.FakeCF   = nil
        end

        local function setFakePos(state)
            if _fpState.Enabled == state then return end
            _fpState.Enabled = state
            getgenv().FakePositionEnabled    = state
            getgenv().HideUndergroundEnabled = state
            local char = _fpPlayers.LocalPlayer and _fpPlayers.LocalPlayer.Character
            local hrp  = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if state then
                if hrp then _fpState.VirtualCF = hrp.CFrame end
                if _fpState.HideMeshes      then _fpCaptureOriginal() end
                if _fpState.AnimationDesync then _fpPlayAnimDesync()   end
                pcall(function() _fpRS:UnbindFromRenderStep("_GravFakePosCam") end)
                _fpRS:BindToRenderStep("_GravFakePosCam", 201, function()
                    if _fpState.Enabled and _fpState.SurfaceCamera then
                        local cam   = workspace.CurrentCamera
                        local depth = _fpState.Depth or 5.8
                        if cam and depth > 0 then cam.CFrame = cam.CFrame + Vector3.new(0, depth, 0) end
                    end
                end)
            else
                pcall(function() _fpRS:UnbindFromRenderStep("_GravFakePosCam") end)
                _fpRestoreOriginal()
                _fpStopAnimDesync()
                _fpUpdateGhostVisualizer()
                if hrp and _fpState.VirtualCF then
                    pcall(function()
                        if _fpState.ZeroVelocity then
                            hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity  = Vector3.zero
                            hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
                        end
                        hrp.CFrame = _fpState.VirtualCF
                    end)
                end
                _fpState.VirtualCF    = nil
                _fpState.HandoffTicks = 0
                _fpClearDesync()
            end
        end

        -- 对外 API（UI 控件回调用）
        getgenv().SetFakePosition         = setFakePos
        getgenv().ToggleFakePosition      = function() setFakePos(not _fpState.Enabled) return _fpState.Enabled end
        getgenv().SetFakePositionDepth    = function(d) if type(d)=="number" then _fpState.Depth=d end end
        getgenv().SetFakePosWalkMode      = function(v) _fpState.WalkMode=v end
        getgenv().SetFakePosSurfaceCamera = function(v) _fpState.SurfaceCamera=v end
        getgenv().SetFakePosAutoNoclip    = function(v) _fpState.AutoNoclip=v end
        getgenv().SetFakePosZeroVelocity  = function(v) _fpState.ZeroVelocity=v end
        getgenv().SetFakePosHideMeshes    = function(v)
            _fpState.HideMeshes=v
            if not v then _fpRestoreOriginal() elseif _fpState.Enabled then _fpCaptureOriginal() end
        end
        getgenv().SetFakePosAnimDesync    = function(v)
            _fpState.AnimationDesync=v
            if not v then _fpStopAnimDesync() elseif _fpState.Enabled then _fpPlayAnimDesync() end
        end
        getgenv().SetFakePosVisualizerOn  = function(v) _fpState.VisualizerEnabled=v; _fpUpdateGhostVisualizer() end
        getgenv().SetFakePosVisualizerColor = function(c) _fpState.VisualizerColor=c end
        getgenv().SetFakePosPanic         = function(v) _fpState.Panic=v end
        getgenv().SetFakePosPanicMinX     = function(v) _fpState.PanicMinX=v end
        getgenv().SetFakePosPanicMaxX     = function(v) _fpState.PanicMaxX=v end
        getgenv().SetFakePosPanicZ        = function(v) _fpState.PanicZ=v end
        -- ▲▲▲ FakePos 新增：状态块 + 辅助函数

        local CDFIELDS = {
            'Cooldown','AttackCooldown','UseDelay','SpinCooldown',
            'HeavyAttackCooldown','AbilityCooldown','FireCooldown','DashCooldown',
        }

        local function zeroCDs()
            task.spawn(function()
                local mods = Services.ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild('Modules')
                local lib  = mods and mods:FindFirstChild('ItemLibrary')
                if lib then
                    local ok, t = pcall(require, lib)
                    if ok and type(t) == 'table' then
                        local items = t.Items or t.Weapons or t.Melee or t.Utilities
                        if type(items) == 'table' then
                            for _, item in pairs(items) do
                                if type(item) == 'table' then
                                    local isM = item.Type == 'Melee'
                                             or item.Category == 'Melee'
                                             or (item.Damage and not item.AmmoType)
                                    if isM then
                                        for _, f in ipairs(CDFIELDS) do
                                            if item[f] ~= nil then item[f] = 0 end
                                        end
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end)
            local char = player.Character
            if char then
                for _, v in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
                    for _, fn in ipairs(CDFIELDS) do
                        if v.Name == fn and (v:IsA('NumberValue') or v:IsA('IntValue')) then
                            v.Value = 0
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end

        -- ── 懒加载缓存 ───────────────────────────────
        local cache = {}

        local function getUtility()
            if cache.util then return cache.util end
            local ok, res = pcall(function() return Modules.shared('Utility') end)
            if ok and type(res) == 'table' then cache.util = res end
            return cache.util
        end
        local function getEnums()
            if cache.enums then return cache.enums end
            local ok, res = pcall(function() return Modules.shared('EnumLibrary') end)
            if ok and type(res) == 'table' then cache.enums = res end
            return cache.enums
        end
        local function getFighters()
            if cache.fighters then return cache.fighters end
            local ok, res = pcall(function() return Modules.controller('FighterController') end)
            if ok and type(res) == 'table' then cache.fighters = res end
            return cache.fighters
        end

        local function getUpdateCamRemote()
            if cache.ucr and cache.ucr.Parent then return cache.ucr end
            local r = Services.ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild('Remotes')
            r = r and r:FindFirstChild('Replication')
            r = r and r:FindFirstChild('Fighter')
            r = r and r:FindFirstChild('UpdateCameraRotation')
            cache.ucr = r
            return r
        end
        local function getUseItemRemote()
            if cache.use and cache.use.Parent then return cache.use end
            local r = Services.ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild('Remotes')
            r = r and r:FindFirstChild('Replication')
            r = r and r:FindFirstChild('Fighter')
            r = r and r:FindFirstChild('UseItem')
            cache.use = r
            return r
        end

        -- ── 目标 / 辅助 ──────────────────────────────
        local function getClosestTarget()
            local char = player.Character
            if not char then return nil, nil, nil end
            local myRoot = char:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart')
            if not myRoot then return nil, nil, nil end
            local myTeam = player:GetAttribute('TeamID')
            local cp, cr, ch, cd = nil, nil, nil, 500
            for _, p in Services.Players:GetPlayers() do
                if p ~= player and (myTeam == nil or p:GetAttribute('TeamID') ~= myTeam) then
                    local pc = p.Character
                    if pc then
                        local pr  = pc:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart')
                        local ph  = pc:FindFirstChild('Head')
                        local phm = pc:FindFirstChildWhichIsA('Humanoid')
                        if pr and ph and phm and phm.Health > 0 then
                            local d = (myRoot.Position - pr.Position).Magnitude
                            if d < cd then cd = d; cp = p; cr = pr; ch = ph end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
            return cp, cr, ch
        end

        local function hasKnifeViewModel(targetPlayer)
            if not targetPlayer then return false end
            local vm = Services.Workspace:FindFirstChild('ViewModels')
            if not vm then return false end
            for _, m in vm:GetChildren() do
                if m:IsA('Model')
                   and string.find(m.Name, targetPlayer.Name, 1, true)
                   and string.find(m.Name, 'Knife', 1, true) then
                    return true
                end
            end
            return false
        end

        local function calcYaw(targetRoot)
            local char = player.Character
            local myRoot = char and char:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart')
            if not myRoot or not targetRoot then return 0 end
            local dx  = targetRoot.Position.X - myRoot.Position.X
            local dz  = targetRoot.Position.Z - myRoot.Position.Z
            local mag = math.sqrt(dx*dx + dz*dz)
            if mag < 0.01 then return 0 end
            return math.atan2(-(dx/mag), -(dz/mag))
        end

        local function equipSlot(slot)
            local fighters = getFighters()
            if not fighters then return end
            local lf = fighters.LocalFighter
            if not lf then return end
            pcall(function() lf:EquipItem(slot) end)
        end

        -- ── 攻击逻辑 ─────────────────────────────────
        local Hvh = {}
        local state = {
            lastFire      = 0,
            lastMeleeFire = 0,
        }

        local function attackStep(settings)
            local cfg = settings()
            if not cfg.enabled then return end

            local util  = getUtility()
            local enums = getEnums()
            if not util or not enums then return end

            -- 刀模式：身后贴人 + 背刺 yaw + StartShooting(nil)
            if cfg.weaponSlot == 'beat ue' then
                local targetPlayer, targetRoot, targetHead = getClosestTarget()
                if not (targetPlayer and targetRoot and targetHead) then return end

                local char = player.Character
                local myRoot = char and char:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart')
                if not myRoot then return end

                local behindPos = targetRoot.Position
                                + targetRoot.CFrame.LookVector * 1.5
                                - Vector3.new(0, 2.0, 0)
                local snapCF = CFrame.new(behindPos, targetHead.Position)

                -- ▼▼▼ Step 1（删 desync）：只写目标位置，不再还原 → 客户端也真的在目标前面
local reps = math.min(10000, math.max(1, math.floor(cfg.followSpeed or 1000)))
local tinyBack = CFrame.new(0, 0, -0.001)
for i = 1, reps do
    if i % 2 == 0 then
        myRoot.CFrame = desyncCF * tinyBack
    else
        myRoot.CFrame = desyncCF
    end
end
myRoot.AssemblyLinearVelocity  = Vector3.zero
myRoot.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
-- ▲▲▲ Step 1 结束

                local fakeYaw = calcYaw(targetRoot) + math.pi
                local ok, encoded = pcall(function()
                    return util:EncodeCameraRotation(Vector2.new(0, fakeYaw))
                end)
                if ok and encoded then
                    local ucr = getUpdateCamRemote()
                    if ucr then
                        pcall(function() ucr:FireServer(encoded, nil) end)
                    end
                end

                local now  = tick()
                local lf   = getFighters() and getFighters().LocalFighter
                local item = lf and lf.EquippedItem
                if item then
                    local floor = math.max(
                        (item.Info and item.Info.AttackDelay) or 0,
                        MeleeCfg.MeleeMinDelay or 0.05
                    )
                    if now - state.lastMeleeFire >= floor then
                        state.lastMeleeFire = now
                        local oid       = item.Get and item:Get('ObjectID')
                        local shootEnum = enums:ToEnum('StartShooting')
                        local remote    = getUseItemRemote()
                        if oid and shootEnum and remote then
                            pcall(function()
                                remote:FireServer(oid, shootEnum, nil, nil)
                            end)
                        end
                    end
                end
                return
            end

            -- 非刀模式：desync + 静默开火
            local targetPlayer, targetRoot, targetHead = getClosestTarget()
            if not (targetPlayer and targetRoot and targetHead) then return end

            local char = player.Character
            local myRoot = char and char:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart')
            if not myRoot then return end

            -- ▼▼▼ Step 3-A：offset 由 followDistance 控制
-- ▼▼▼ Step 1（方向翻转版）：offset 由 followDistance 控制，位置在目标"前面"
local dist = tonumber(cfg.followDistance) or 1.5
-- ▼▼▼ Step 2-C：前后由 followFront 决定
local signedDist = (cfg.followFront and -dist or dist)
local desyncPos
if hasKnifeViewModel(targetPlayer) then
    local knifeZ = (cfg.followFront and -1.5 or 1.5)
    desyncPos = (targetRoot.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 1 + dist * 3, knifeZ)).Position
else
    desyncPos = (targetRoot.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 1, signedDist)).Position
end
local desyncCF = CFrame.lookAt(desyncPos, targetHead.Position)
-- ▲▲▲ Step 2-C 结束
local desyncCF = CFrame.lookAt(desyncPos, targetHead.Position)
-- ▲▲▲ Step 1 结束
-- ▲▲▲ Step 3-A 结束

-- ▼▼▼ Step 2（删 desync）：只写目标位置，不再还原
myRoot.CFrame = snapCF
myRoot.AssemblyLinearVelocity  = Vector3.zero
myRoot.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
-- ▲▲▲ Step 2 结束

            if tick() - state.lastFire < cfg.fireRate then return end
            state.lastFire = tick()

            local lf   = getFighters() and getFighters().LocalFighter
            if not lf then return end
            local item = lf.EquippedItem
            if not item then return end

            local rng = Vector3.new(
                (math.random() - 0.5) * 0.1,
                (math.random() - 0.5) * 0.1,
                (math.random() - 0.5) * 0.1)
            local aimedPos  = targetHead.Position + rng
            local aimCF     = CFrame.lookAt(desyncCF.Position, aimedPos)
            local targetCF  = targetHead.CFrame
            local objOffset = targetHead.CFrame:ToObjectSpace(CFrame.new(aimedPos))

            local cameradata = {}
            cameradata[utf8.char(1)] = {
                [utf8.char(0)] = util:EncodeCFrame(aimCF),
                [utf8.char(1)] = util:EncodeCFrame(targetCF),
                [utf8.char(2)] = targetHead,
                [utf8.char(3)] = util:EncodeCFrame(objOffset),
            }

            local remote     = getUseItemRemote()
            local oid        = item.Get and item:Get('ObjectID')
            local shootEnum  = enums:ToEnum('StartShooting')
            if remote and oid and shootEnum then
                pcall(function()
                    remote:FireServer(oid, shootEnum, cameradata, nil)
                end)
            end
        end

        function Hvh.start(run, settings)
            local cfg    = settings()
            local slotMap = { Primary = 1, Secondary = 2, Melee = 3, ['beat ue'] = 3 }
            equipSlot(slotMap[cfg.weaponSlot] or 1)

                        local EvEngine = __ANTIBRAIN_MODULES.bz()
            EvEngine.resetEvasion()

            local conn = Services.RunService.Stepped:Connect(function()
                -- ▼▼▼ Evasion Hook 新增
                local evCfg = settings().evasion
                if evCfg and evCfg.mode ~= 'Off' then
                    local root = player.Character
                        and player.Character:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart')
                    if root then
                        local evCF = EvEngine.computeEvasion(root, evCfg, nil)
                        if evCF then
                            pcall(function()
                                root.CFrame = evCF
                            end)
                        end
                    end
                end
                -- ▲▲▲ Evasion Hook 新增
                local ok, err = pcall(attackStep, settings)
                if not ok then
                    run:report(string.format('HvH step failed: %s', tostring(err)))
                end
            end)
            run:add(function() conn:Disconnect() end)
            run:add(function()
                Services.RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep('__hvh_restore')
                Services.RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep('__hvh_knife_restore')
            end)

            -- ▼▼▼ FakePos 新增：主 Heartbeat
            local fpHb = Services.RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
                local fp = settings().fakePos
                if not fp then return end

                -- 同步所有开关到 _fpState
                _fpState.Depth          = fp.depth
                _fpState.WalkMode       = fp.walkMode
                _fpState.SurfaceCamera  = fp.surfaceCamera
                _fpState.AutoNoclip     = fp.autoNoclip
                _fpState.ZeroVelocity   = fp.zeroVelocity
                _fpState.HideMeshes     = fp.hideMeshes
                _fpState.AnimationDesync = fp.animDesync
                _fpState.VisualizerEnabled = fp.ghostBox
                _fpState.Panic          = fp.panic
                _fpState.PanicMinX      = fp.panicMinX
                _fpState.PanicMaxX      = fp.panicMaxX
                _fpState.PanicZ         = fp.panicZ

                -- 总开关变化时调用 setFakePos 以触发开关副作用
                if fp.enabled ~= _fpState.Enabled then
                    setFakePos(fp.enabled)
                end

                if not _fpState.Enabled then return end

                local char = _fpPlayers.LocalPlayer and _fpPlayers.LocalPlayer.Character
                if not char then return end
                local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                if not hrp or not hum then return end

                if _fpState.HandoffTicks > 0 then
                    _fpState.HandoffTicks -= 1
                    if _fpState.VirtualCF then
                        hrp.CFrame = _fpState.VirtualCF
                        _fpSetDesync("handoff", _fpState.VirtualCF, hrp.CFrame)
                    end
                    return
                end

                if not _fpState.VirtualCF then _fpState.VirtualCF = hrp.CFrame end

                local cam     = workspace.CurrentCamera
                local camLook = cam and cam.CFrame.LookVector or Vector3.new(0, 0, -1)
                local camYaw  = math.atan2(-camLook.X, -camLook.Z)
                local surfRot = CFrame.Angles(0, camYaw, 0)

                if _fpState.WalkMode then
                    local moveDir = hum.MoveDirection
                    if moveDir.Magnitude > 0 then
                        _fpState.VirtualCF = CFrame.new(
                            _fpState.VirtualCF.Position + moveDir * (hum.WalkSpeed * dt)
                        ) * surfRot
                    else
                        _fpState.VirtualCF = CFrame.new(_fpState.VirtualCF.Position) * surfRot
                    end
                else
                    _fpState.VirtualCF = CFrame.new(_fpState.VirtualCF.Position) * surfRot
                end

                local depth = _fpState.Depth or 5.8
                local ugPos = _fpState.VirtualCF.Position - Vector3.new(0, depth, 0)

                -- Panic Jitter：每帧随机偏移 X（及可选 Z）
                if _fpState.Panic then
                    local mnX = math.min(_fpState.PanicMinX, _fpState.PanicMaxX)
                    local mxX = math.max(_fpState.PanicMinX, _fpState.PanicMaxX)
                    local jx  = mnX + math.random() * (mxX - mnX)
                    local jz  = _fpState.PanicZ and ((math.random() - 0.5) * (mxX - mnX)) or 0
                    ugPos = ugPos + Vector3.new(jx, 0, jz)
                end

                local pitchCF = CFrame.Angles(0, math.rad(_fpState.CustomYaw or 0), 0)

                if _fpState.AutoNoclip then
                    for _, p in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
                        if p:IsA("BasePart") and p.CanCollide then p.CanCollide = false end
                    end
                end

                if _fpState.ZeroVelocity then
                    hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity  = Vector3.zero
                    hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
                end

                hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(ugPos) * surfRot * pitchCF
                _fpSetDesync("fake", _fpState.VirtualCF, hrp.CFrame)
                _fpUpdateGhostVisualizer()
            end)

            -- 网格透明度强制 Heartbeat
            local fpMeshHb = Services.RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
                if not _fpState.Enabled or not _fpState.HideMeshes then return end
                if not _fpState.OriginalParts then return end
                for _, data in ipairs(_fpState.OriginalParts) do
                    local p = data.part
                    if p and p.Parent and p.Transparency ~= 1 then
                        pcall(function() p.Transparency = 1 end)
                    end
                end
            end)

            -- 卸载时断连 + 清理 FakePos
            run:add(function()
                fpHb:Disconnect()
                fpMeshHb:Disconnect()
                pcall(function() Services.RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep("_GravFakePosCam") end)
                setFakePos(false)
            end)
            -- ▲▲▲ FakePos 新增：主 Heartbeat
        end

        -- ▼▼▼ GV9 Ragebot 引擎
        local GV9 = {}

        -- 状态变量
        local _gvLastFire      = 0
        local _gvHoldUntil     = 0
        local _gvDsRoot        = nil
        local _gvDsCF          = nil
        local _gvDsVel         = Vector3.zero
        local _gvDsRot         = Vector3.zero
        local _gvDsBound       = false
        local _gvPing          = 0.06
        local _gvPingAt        = 0
        local _gvDeflecting    = {}
        local _gvPlCache       = {}
        local _gvPlCacheAt     = 0
        local VOID_RADIUS      = 9000
        local EYE_OFFSET       = Vector3.new(0, 1.5, 0)
        local IMMUNE_ATTRS     = {"IsInvincible","Immune","IsImmune","SpawnProtection"}
        local K0,K1,K2,K3      = utf8.char(0),utf8.char(1),utf8.char(2),utf8.char(3)

        local function _gvIsImmune(pl)
            local ch = pl.Character
            if ch then
                if ch:FindFirstChildOfClass("ForceField") then return true end
                for _, a in ipairs(IMMUNE_ATTRS) do if ch:GetAttribute(a)==true then return true end end
            end
            for _, a in ipairs(IMMUNE_ATTRS) do if pl:GetAttribute(a)==true then return true end end
            return false
        end

        local function _gvIsEnemy(pl)
            if pl == player then return false end
            local pt = pl:GetAttribute("TeamID"); local lt = player:GetAttribute("TeamID")
            if pt and lt then return pt ~= lt end
            return true
        end

        local function _gvInFov(hrp, fovR)
            if fovR >= 9999 then return true end
            local cam = Services.Workspace.CurrentCamera
            local p3, onScreen = cam:WorldToViewportPoint(hrp.Position)
            if not onScreen then return false end
            local mouse = Services.UserInputService:GetMouseLocation()
            return (Vector2.new(p3.X, p3.Y) - mouse).Magnitude <= fovR
        end

        local function _gvHasKnife(pl)
            local vm = Services.Workspace:FindFirstChild("ViewModels"); if not vm then return false end
            for _, m in ipairs(vm:GetChildren()) do
                if m:IsA("Model")
                   and string.find(m.Name, pl.Name, 1, true)
                   and string.find(m.Name, "Knife", 1, true) then return true end
            end
            return false
        end

        local function _gvLeadPos(hrp, extrapOn)
            if not extrapOn then return hrp.Position end
            local now = os.clock()
            if now - _gvPingAt >= 1 then
                _gvPingAt = now
                pcall(function() _gvPing = math.clamp(player:GetNetworkPing(), 0, 0.5) end)
            end
            local vel = Vector3.zero
            pcall(function() vel = hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity end)
            if vel.Magnitude < 0.05 then return hrp.Position end
            local spd  = vel.Magnitude
            local look = (_gvPing*1.2+0.016)*(1+math.clamp(spd/40,0,1.6)*0.4)
            return hrp.Position + vel * math.clamp(look, 0, 0.5)
        end

        local function _gvTarget(cfg)
            local now = os.clock()
            if now - _gvPlCacheAt > 0.5 then
                _gvPlCacheAt = now; _gvPlCache = {}
                for _, pl in ipairs(Services.Players:GetPlayers()) do
                    if pl ~= player and pl.Character then
                        local h = pl.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                        local hm = pl.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                        if h and hm and hm.Health > 0 then table.insert(_gvPlCache, pl.Character) end
                    end
                end
            end
            local myChar = player.Character
            local myHRP  = myChar and myChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if not myHRP then return nil, nil, nil end
            local bPl, bHRP, bHead, bD = nil, nil, nil, math.huge
            for _, ch in ipairs(_gvPlCache) do
                local pl = Services.Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(ch)
                if not pl or not _gvIsEnemy(pl) or _gvIsImmune(pl) then continue end
                local hrp  = ch:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                local head = ch:FindFirstChild("Head")
                local hm   = ch:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                if not hrp or not head or not hm or hm.Health <= 0 then continue end
                if not _gvInFov(hrp, cfg.fov) then continue end
                local d = (myHRP.Position - hrp.Position).Magnitude
                if d < bD then bPl, bHRP, bHead, bD = pl, hrp, head, d end
            end
            return bPl, bHRP, bHead
        end

        local function _gvVoidCF()
            local u = math.random(); local v = math.random()
            local t = math.pi*2*u; local p = math.acos(math.clamp(2*v-1,-1,1))
            local pos = Vector3.new(
                VOID_RADIUS*math.sin(p)*math.cos(t),
                VOID_RADIUS*math.cos(p),
                VOID_RADIUS*math.sin(p)*math.sin(t))
            return CFrame.lookAt(pos, Vector3.zero)
        end

        local function _gvFlushDs()
            local root, cf = _gvDsRoot, _gvDsCF
            _gvDsRoot, _gvDsCF = nil, nil
            if root and root.Parent and cf then
                pcall(function()
                    root.CFrame = cf
                    root.AssemblyLinearVelocity  = _gvDsVel
                    root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = _gvDsRot
                end)
            end
        end

        local function _gvApplyDs(desyncCF)
            local myChar = player.Character
            local myRoot = myChar and myChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if not myRoot or not myRoot:IsA("BasePart") then return false end
            if _gvDsCF == nil or _gvDsRoot ~= myRoot then
                _gvDsRoot = myRoot
                _gvDsCF   = myRoot.CFrame
                _gvDsVel  = myRoot.AssemblyLinearVelocity
                _gvDsRot  = myRoot.AssemblyAngularVelocity
            end
            myRoot.CFrame = desyncCF
            return true
        end

        local function _gvItemCD(item)
            local cd = 0
            pcall(function()
                local info  = item.Info; if type(info) ~= "table" then return end
                local c     = tonumber(info.ShootCooldown) or 0
                local burst = tonumber(info.BurstCount) or 1
                local bcd   = tonumber(info.BurstCooldown)
                if burst > 1 and bcd then c = math.min(c, bcd) end
                cd = math.clamp(c, 0, 5)
            end)
            return cd
        end

        local function _gvPellets(item)
            local n = 1
            pcall(function()
                local c = item.Info and item.Info.ShootPellets
                if type(c) == "number" then n = math.max(1, math.floor(c)) end
            end)
            return n
        end

        local function _gvFire(cfg, tPl, tHRP, tHead)
            local util  = getUtility()
            local enums = getEnums()
            local fc    = getFighters()
            local remote = getUseItemRemote()
            if not (util and enums and fc and remote) then return end
            local lf = fc.LocalFighter; if not lf then return end
            local item = lf.EquippedItem; if not item then return end
            local oid; pcall(function() oid = item:Get("ObjectID") end)
            if not oid then return end

            local ch  = tHRP and tHRP.Parent
            if not ch then return end
            local hm = ch:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if not hm or hm.Health <= 0 then return end

            -- knife deflect guard
            if cfg.knifeCheck and _gvDeflecting[tPl] then return end

            local targetPart = cfg.aimHead
                and (ch:FindFirstChild("HitboxHead") or tHead)
                or  (ch:FindFirstChild("HitboxBody") or ch:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or tHead)

            local leadPos = _gvLeadPos(tHRP, cfg.extrap)
            local hitPt   = targetPart.Position

            local knifeHolder = _gvHasKnife(tPl)
            local desyncCF
            if knifeHolder then
                desyncCF = CFrame.lookAt(tHRP.Position + Vector3.new(0,-5,0), leadPos)
            else
                local facing = tHRP.CFrame.LookVector
                local flat   = Vector3.new(facing.X, 0, facing.Z)
                if flat.Magnitude < 0.05 then flat = Vector3.new(0,0,-1) end
                desyncCF = CFrame.lookAt(tHRP.Position + flat.Unit + Vector3.new(0,1,0), leadPos)
            end

            if cfg.desync then _gvApplyDs(desyncCF) end

            local eye = desyncCF.Position + EYE_OFFSET
            if (eye - leadPos).Magnitude < 0.1 then eye = leadPos + Vector3.new(0,0.2,0) end

            local aimCF    = CFrame.lookAt(eye, leadPos)
            local partCF   = targetPart.CFrame
            local offset   = partCF:ToObjectSpace(CFrame.new(hitPt))

            local encAim, encOff
            local ok = pcall(function()
                encAim = util:EncodeCFrame(aimCF)
                encOff = util:EncodeCFrame(offset)
            end)
            if not ok or not encAim then return end

            local camdata = {
                [K1] = { [K0]=encAim, [K1]=encAim, [K2]=targetPart, [K3]=encOff },
            }
            if _gvPellets(item) > 1 then camdata[K2] = true end

            local startEnum; pcall(function() startEnum = enums:ToEnum("StartShooting") end)
            if not startEnum then return end
            pcall(function() remote:FireServer(oid, startEnum, camdata, nil) end)
        end

        function GV9.start(run, settings)
            _gvHoldUntil = tick() + 1
            run:connect(Services.Players.LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded, function()
                _gvHoldUntil = tick() + 1
            end)

            -- RenderStep 还原（优先级低于 Camera，确保相机不见 desync 位）
            local priority = Enum.RenderPriority.Camera.Value - 1
            Services.RunService:BindToRenderStep("__gv9_ds_restore", priority, function()
                if _gvDsCF then _gvFlushDs() end
            end)
            run:add(function()
                pcall(function() Services.RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep("__gv9_ds_restore") end)
                _gvFlushDs()
            end)

            local hb = Services.RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
                local cfg = settings().gv9Rage
                if not cfg or not cfg.enabled then return end
                if tick() < _gvHoldUntil then return end

                -- 偏转追踪（每 0.1s 刷新）
                pcall(function()
                    local fc = getFighters()
                    if fc and fc.Objects then
                        for _, fo in ipairs(fc.Objects) do
                            local pl = fo.Player; if not pl then continue end
                            local eq = fo.EquippedItem; local vm2 = eq and eq.ViewModel
                            local defl = false
                            if vm2 and vm2.Name == "Katana" then
                                defl = (eq._attack_cooldown and eq._attack_cooldown > tick()) or false
                            end
                            _gvDeflecting[pl] = defl
                        end
                    end
                end)

                local tPl, tHRP, tHead = _gvTarget(cfg)
                if not tPl then return end

                local now    = tick()
                local fireAt = _gvLastFire + cfg.fireRate
                local remain = fireAt - now

                -- void park 冷却期间
                if remain > 0.002 then
                    local lf   = getFighters() and getFighters().LocalFighter
                    local item = lf and lf.EquippedItem
                    if cfg.voidPark and item and _gvItemCD(item) > 0.05 then
                        _gvApplyDs(_gvVoidCF())
                    end
                    return
                end

                _gvLastFire = tick()
                task.spawn(_gvFire, cfg, tPl, tHRP, tHead)
            end)
            run:add(function() hb:Disconnect() end)
        end
        -- ▲▲▲ GV9 Ragebot 引擎

        return table.freeze({
            MeleeCfg = MeleeCfg,
            HvhCfg   = HvhCfg,
            zeroCDs  = zeroCDs,
            Hvh      = Hvh,
            GV9      = GV9,
        })
    end

    function __ANTIBRAIN_MODULES.bw()
        local v = __ANTIBRAIN_MODULES.cache.bw
        if not v then
            v = { c = __modImpl() }
            __ANTIBRAIN_MODULES.cache.bw = v
        end
        return v.c
    end
end
-- ============================================================
-- ▲▲▲ Step 1 结束
-- ============================================================

-- ▼▼▼ Evasion Engine 新增：独立引擎模块 __ANTIBRAIN_MODULES.bz
do
    local function __modImpl()
        local Services = __ANTIBRAIN_MODULES.k()
        local Random   = Random.new()

        -- ── 辅助：正弦振荡深度 ────────────────────────────
        local function oscillate(minV, maxV, freq)
            local n = 6.283185307 * freq
            return minV + (maxV - minV) * (math.sin(os.clock() * n) + 1) * 0.5
        end

        -- ── Random Evasion：极值轴传送 ────────────────────
        local function computeRandom(rootPos, cfg)
            local baseR   = cfg.randomRadius
            local n       = baseR + baseR * cfg.randomFactor
            local anchor  = cfg.randomCharOrigin
                            and rootPos
                            or  Vector3.new(0, rootPos.Y, 0)
            local offset  = Random:NextUnitVector() * (baseR + Random:NextNumber() * (n - baseR))
            local pt      = anchor + offset
            -- 始终极值化 Y（高空），避免触碰地面 OOB
            return CFrame.new(pt.X, 1073741824, pt.Z)
        end

        -- ── ProjectileBreaker：表面藏身 ───────────────────
        local _pbPool       = {}
        local _pbProcessed  = {}
        local _pbNextCooldown = -1
        local _pbLastSurface  = nil
        local _overlapParams  = OverlapParams.new()
        _overlapParams.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
        _overlapParams.BruteForceAllSlow = true

        local function isKillGeometry(part)
            return part.Name == "Barriers"
                or part:HasTag("OutOfBoundsPart")
                or part:HasTag("KillBrick")
        end

        local function overlapsKill(pos)
            for _, p in workspace:GetPartBoundsInBox(
                CFrame.new(pos), Vector3.new(5,5,5), _overlapParams) do
                if isKillGeometry(p) then return true end
            end
            return false
        end

        local function tryGetSurface(part)
            if part.Transparency == 1 then return nil end
            local sz = part.Size
            if sz.X * sz.Y * sz.Z < 64 then return nil end
            local n = sz.Magnitude + 1000
            for _ = 1, 5 do
                local origin = part.Position + Random:NextUnitVector() * n
                local result = workspace:Raycast(origin, part.Position - origin)
                if result and result.Normal.Y >= 0.98 then
                    local normal = result.Normal
                    local hit    = result.Position
                    local refV   = part.Position - hit
                    local sideV  = refV - normal * refV:Dot(normal)
                    if sideV.Magnitude < 0.0001 then
                        sideV = part.CFrame.LookVector
                        sideV = sideV - normal * sideV:Dot(normal)
                    end
                    local right   = sideV.Unit:Cross(normal).Unit
                    local forward = normal:Cross(right).Unit
                    return { pos = hit, up = normal, right = right, forward = forward }
                end
            end
            return nil
        end

        local function pbComputeCFrame(surf, cfg)
            local depUp  = oscillate(cfg.pbDepUpMin,  cfg.pbDepUpMax,  cfg.pbUpFreq)
            local depFwd = oscillate(cfg.pbDepFwdMin, cfg.pbDepFwdMax, cfg.pbFwdFreq)
            local origin = surf.pos
                         - surf.up * 0.01
                         - surf.up * depUp
                         + surf.forward * depFwd
            return CFrame.fromMatrix(origin, surf.right, surf.up, -surf.forward)
        end

        local function pbFallback(rootPos, cfg)
            local baseR  = cfg.pbFallbackRadius
            local n      = baseR + baseR * cfg.pbFallbackFactor
            local anchor = cfg.pbFallbackCharOrigin
                           and rootPos
                           or  Vector3.new(0, rootPos.Y, 0)
            local offset = Random:NextUnitVector() * (baseR + Random:NextNumber() * (n - baseR))
            local pt     = anchor + offset
            local axis   = Random:NextInteger(1, 3)
            local x, y, z = pt.X, pt.Y, pt.Z
            if axis == 1 then x = 1073741824
            elseif axis == 2 then y = 1073741824
            else z = 1073741824 end
            return CFrame.new(x, y, z)
        end

        local function pbScanParts(envTag)
            local CS   = game:GetService("CollectionService")
            local count = 0
            for _, part in CS:GetTagged("RaycastWhitelist" .. tostring(envTag)) do
                if isKillGeometry(part) then continue end
                if _pbProcessed[part] then continue end
                _pbProcessed[part] = true
                count += 1
                local surf = tryGetSurface(part)
                if surf then
                    table.insert(_pbPool, surf)
                end
                if count >= 64 or #_pbPool >= 30 then return end
                for _, child in part:GetDescendants() do
                    if isKillGeometry(child) then continue end
                    if _pbProcessed[child] then continue end
                    if not child:IsA("BasePart") then continue end
                    _pbProcessed[child] = true
                    count += 1
                    local csurf = tryGetSurface(child)
                    if csurf then table.insert(_pbPool, csurf) end
                    if count >= 64 or #_pbPool >= 30 then return end
                end
            end
        end

        -- ── 公开 Compute ───────────────────────────────────
        local function computeEvasion(rootPart, cfg, envTag)
            local mode = cfg.mode
            if mode == 'Off' or not mode then return nil end

            if mode == 'Random' then
                return computeRandom(rootPart.Position, cfg)
            end

            if mode == 'ProjectileBreaker' then
                local now = os.clock()
                if now < _pbNextCooldown and _pbLastSurface then
                    return pbComputeCFrame(_pbLastSurface, cfg)
                end
                if #_pbPool < 30 and envTag then
                    pbScanParts(envTag)
                end
                if #_pbPool >= 30 then
                    _pbNextCooldown = now + cfg.pbReposInterval
                    _pbLastSurface  = _pbPool[Random:NextInteger(1, #_pbPool)]
                    return pbComputeCFrame(_pbLastSurface, cfg)
                end
                return pbFallback(rootPart.Position, cfg)
            end

            return nil
        end

        local function resetEvasion()
            _pbPool          = {}
            _pbProcessed     = {}
            _pbNextCooldown  = -1
            _pbLastSurface   = nil
        end

        return table.freeze({
            computeEvasion = computeEvasion,
            resetEvasion   = resetEvasion,
        })
    end

    function __ANTIBRAIN_MODULES.bz()
        local v = __ANTIBRAIN_MODULES.cache.bz
        if not v then
            v = { c = __modImpl() }
            __ANTIBRAIN_MODULES.cache.bz = v
        end
        return v.c
    end
end
-- ▲▲▲ Evasion Engine 新增

-- ▼▼▼ bx: Mod Detector
do
    local function __modImpl()
        local Services = __ANTIBRAIN_MODULES.k()
        __ANTIBRAIN_MODULES.c()

        local player = Services.Players.LocalPlayer
        local GroupService = Services.GroupService

        local STAFF_WORDS = { "mod", "admin", "staff", "dev", "owner", "founder", "manager", "support", "contractor" }

        local State = {
            enabled = false,
            groupId = nil,
            staffRank = 200,
            staff = {},
            ranks = {},
            friends = {},
            rolesReady = false,
            loading = false,
            kicked = false,
        }

        local function playerKey(p)
            local ok, id = pcall(function() return p.UserId end)
            return (ok and type(id) == "number") and id or nil
        end

        local function getJson(url)
            local ok, body = pcall(function() return game:HttpGet(url) end)
            if not ok or type(body) ~= "string" then return nil end
            local dok, data = pcall(function() return Services.HttpService:JSONDecode(body) end)
            return dok and data or nil
        end

        local function isStaffRole(name)
            local lower = string.lower(name)
            for _, word in STAFF_WORDS do
                if string.find(lower, word, 1, true) then return true end
            end
            return false
        end

        local function loadRoleMembers(gid, roleId)
            local cursor = ""
            for _ = 1, 10 do
                local suffix = if cursor ~= "" then "&cursor=" .. cursor else ""
                local page = getJson(string.format(
                    "https://groups.roblox.com/v1/groups/%d/roles/%d/users?limit=100&sortOrder=Asc%s",
                    gid, roleId, suffix))
                if type(page) ~= "table" or type(page.data) ~= "table" then return end
                for _, user in page.data do
                    local id = tonumber(user.userId)
                    if id then
                        State.staff[id] = tostring(user.username or user.displayName or id)
                    end
                end
                cursor = page.nextPageCursor
                if type(cursor) ~= "string" or cursor == "" then return end
            end
        end

        local function loadStaff()
            if State.rolesReady or State.loading then return end
            State.loading = true
            if game.CreatorType == Enum.CreatorType.Group then
                State.groupId = game.CreatorId
            end
            local gid = State.groupId
            if gid then
                local payload = getJson(string.format("https://groups.roblox.com/v1/groups/%d/roles", gid))
                local roles = if type(payload) == "table" then payload.roles else nil
                if type(roles) ~= "table" then
                    local ok, info = pcall(GroupService.GetGroupInfoAsync, GroupService, gid)
                    roles = if ok and type(info) == "table" then info.Roles else nil
                end
                local lowest = math.huge
                for _, role in (if type(roles) == "table" then roles else {}) do
                    local name = tostring(role.name or role.Name or "")
                    local rank = tonumber(role.rank or role.Rank) or 0
                    if rank > 1 and isStaffRole(name) then
                        lowest = math.min(lowest, rank)
                        local roleId = tonumber(role.id)
                        if roleId then loadRoleMembers(gid, roleId) end
                    end
                end
                if lowest < math.huge then State.staffRank = lowest end
            end
            State.rolesReady = true
            State.loading = false
        end

        local function rankOf(p)
            local id = playerKey(p)
            if not id then return 0 end
            local cached = State.ranks[id]
            if cached then return cached end
            local rank = 0
            local attr = p:GetAttribute("GroupRank")
            if type(attr) == "number" then
                rank = attr
            elseif State.groupId then
                local ok, v = pcall((p :: any).GetRankInGroup, p, State.groupId)
                if ok and type(v) == "number" then rank = v end
            end
            State.ranks[id] = rank
            return rank
        end

        local function isModerator(p)
            local id = playerKey(p)
            return (id ~= nil and State.staff[id] ~= nil)
                or rankOf(p) >= State.staffRank
                or p:GetAttribute("IsRobloxEmployee") == true
        end

        local function friendOfMod(p)
            if next(State.staff) == nil then return nil end
            local id = playerKey(p)
            if not id then return nil end
            local set = State.friends[id]
            if set == nil then
                set = {}
                local data = getJson(string.format("https://friends.roblox.com/v1/users/%d/friends", id))
                if type(data) == "table" and type(data.data) == "table" then
                    for _, friend in data.data do
                        local friendId = tonumber(friend.id)
                        if friendId then set[friendId] = true end
                    end
                end
                State.friends[id] = set
            end
            for friendId in set do
                local name = State.staff[friendId]
                if name then return name end
            end
            return nil
        end

        local function kickFor(p, modName, viaFriend)
            if State.kicked then return end
            State.kicked = true
            local reason = if viaFriend
                then string.format("friend of moderator %s (@%s)", modName, p.Name)
                else string.format("moderator %s (@%s)", p.DisplayName, p.Name)
            pcall(function() player:Kick("[Arch] mod detector: " .. reason) end)
        end

        local function scan()
            if not State.enabled or State.kicked then return end
            loadStaff()
            local others = {}
            for _, p in Services.Players:GetPlayers() do
                if p ~= player then table.insert(others, p) end
            end
            for _, p in others do
                if not State.enabled then return end
                if isModerator(p) then kickFor(p, p.Name, false); return end
            end
            for _, p in others do
                if not State.enabled then return end
                local mn = friendOfMod(p)
                if mn then kickFor(p, mn, true); return end
            end
        end

        local Detector = {}

        function Detector.new(scope)
            scope:connect(Services.Players.PlayerAdded, function()
                task.delay(1, function()
                    if State.enabled then
                        local ok, err = pcall(scan)
                        if not ok then scope:report("mod detector scan: " .. tostring(err)) end
                    end
                end)
            end)
            scope:connect(Services.Players.PlayerRemoving, function(p)
                local id = playerKey(p)
                if id then State.ranks[id] = nil; State.friends[id] = nil end
            end)
            scope:spawn(function()
                while not scope.closed do
                    if State.enabled then
                        local ok, err = pcall(scan)
                        if not ok then scope:report("mod detector scan: " .. tostring(err)) end
                    end
                    task.wait(4)
                end
            end)
        end

        function Detector.setEnabled(v)
            State.enabled = v == true
            State.kicked = false
        end

        function Detector.isEnabled() return State.enabled end
        function Detector.getStaffRank() return State.staffRank end
        function Detector.forceScan()
            local ok, err = pcall(scan)
            if not ok then warn("[Arch] mod detector scan error: " .. tostring(err)) end
        end

        return table.freeze(Detector)
    end

    function __ANTIBRAIN_MODULES.bx()
        local v = __ANTIBRAIN_MODULES.cache.bx
        if not v then
            v = { c = __modImpl() }
            __ANTIBRAIN_MODULES.cache.bx = v
        end
        return v.c
    end
end
-- ▲▲▲ bx

-- ▼▼▼ by: Lua & Servers tools
do
    local function __modImpl()
        local Services = __ANTIBRAIN_MODULES.k()
        __ANTIBRAIN_MODULES.c()

        local LUA_DIR = "luas"

        local function notify(msg) warn("[extras/lua] " .. msg) end

        local Tools = {}

        function Tools.listScripts()
            local out = {}
            pcall(function()
                if not isfolder(LUA_DIR) then makefolder(LUA_DIR) end
                for _, path in listfiles(LUA_DIR) do
                    local base = string.match(tostring(path), "([^/\\]+)$") or tostring(path)
                    local stem = string.match(base, "^(.+)%.lua$") or string.match(base, "^(.+)%.txt$")
                    if stem then
                        table.insert(out, { name = stem, path = LUA_DIR .. "/" .. base })
                    end
                end
            end)
            table.sort(out, function(a, b) return string.lower(a.name) < string.lower(b.name) end)
            return out
        end

        function Tools.runScript(name, path)
            local ok, src = pcall(readfile, path)
            if not ok or type(src) ~= "string" then
                notify("missing · " .. name)
                return
            end
            local loader = loadstring or load
            if type(loader) ~= "function" then
                notify("loadstring unavailable")
                return
            end
            local cok, fn, err = pcall(loader, src, "=" .. name)
            if not cok or type(fn) ~= "function" then
                notify("syntax error · " .. tostring(if cok then err else fn))
                return
            end
            notify("loaded · " .. name)
            task.spawn(function()
                local rok, rerr = pcall(fn)
                if not rok then notify(name .. " · " .. tostring(rerr)) end
            end)
        end

        function Tools.deleteScript(name, path)
            local ok, err = pcall(delfile, path)
            if ok then
                notify("deleted · " .. name)
                return true
            end
            notify("delete failed · " .. tostring(err))
            return false
        end

        function Tools.listServers()
            local rows = {}
            local cursor = ""
            for _ = 1, 3 do
                local suffix = ""
                if cursor ~= "" then
                    local okEnc, enc = pcall(function() return Services.HttpService:UrlEncode(cursor) end)
                    suffix = "&cursor=" .. (okEnc and tostring(enc) or cursor)
                end
                local url = string.format(
                    "https://games.roblox.com/v1/games/%d/servers/Public?sortOrder=Desc&limit=100%s",
                    game.PlaceId, suffix)
                local ok, body = pcall(function() return game:HttpGet(url) end)
                if not ok or type(body) ~= "string" then break end
                local dok, page = pcall(function() return Services.HttpService:JSONDecode(body) end)
                if not dok or type(page) ~= "table" or type(page.data) ~= "table" then break end
                for _, entry in page.data do
                    local id = if type(entry) == "table" then entry.id else nil
                    if type(id) == "string" and id ~= "" and id ~= game.JobId then
                        table.insert(rows, {
                            id = id,
                            playing = tonumber(entry.playing) or 0,
                            maxPlayers = tonumber(entry.maxPlayers) or 0,
                            ping = tonumber(entry.ping),
                        })
                    end
                end
                cursor = if type(page.nextPageCursor) == "string" then page.nextPageCursor else ""
                if cursor == "" then break end
            end
            table.sort(rows, function(a, b) return a.playing > b.playing end)
            return rows
        end

        function Tools.joinServer(jobId)
            if type(jobId) ~= "string" or jobId == "" or jobId == game.JobId then return end
            local TeleportService = Services.TeleportService
            pcall(function()
                TeleportService:TeleportToPlaceInstance(game.PlaceId, jobId, Services.Players.LocalPlayer)
            end)
        end

        return table.freeze(Tools)
    end

    function __ANTIBRAIN_MODULES.by()
        local v = __ANTIBRAIN_MODULES.cache.by
        if not v then
            v = { c = __modImpl() }
            __ANTIBRAIN_MODULES.cache.by = v
        end
        return v.c
    end
end

-- ▼▼▼ bz: Sounds (hit / shoot)
do
    local function __modImpl()
        local Services = __ANTIBRAIN_MODULES.k()

        local HIT_SOUNDS = {
            BowHit     = { SoundId = "rbxassetid://1053296915" },
            Click      = { SoundId = "rbxassetid://1347140027" },
            CSGO       = { SoundId = "rbxassetid://5764885315" },
            Damage     = { SoundId = "rbxassetid://8587183599" },
            Explosion  = { SoundId = "rbxassetid://90854697257230" },
            Gamesense  = { SoundId = "rbxassetid://4817809188" },
            Hover      = { SoundId = "rbxassetid://6333717580" },
            Hitmarker  = { SoundId = "rbxassetid://1129547534" },
            Impact     = { SoundId = "rbxassetid://6759025689" },
            Ice        = { SoundId = "rbxassetid://268934012" },
            Minecraft  = { SoundId = "rbxassetid://4018616850" },
            Neverlose  = { SoundId = "rbxassetid://6607204501" },
            Pop        = { SoundId = "rbxassetid://18803667669" },
            Rust       = { SoundId = "rbxassetid://5043539486" },
            Sparkle    = { SoundId = "rbxassetid://132463144859699" },
            Steve      = { SoundId = "rbxassetid://4965083997" },
            TF2        = { SoundId = "rbxassetid://8255306220" },
            Windows    = { SoundId = "rbxassetid://9066167010" },
        }

        local SHOOT_SOUNDS = {
            Crossbow       = { SoundId = "rbxassetid://8616930816" },
            AK47           = { SoundId = "rbxassetid://801142899" },
            M249           = { SoundId = "rbxassetid://2934844996" },
            PlasmaPistol   = { SoundId = "rbxassetid://5312934759" },
            G36c           = { SoundId = "rbxassetid://4759267374" },
            PlasmaMachine  = { SoundId = "rbxassetid://2605256561" },
            PlasmaShotgun  = { SoundId = "rbxassetid://2605233927" },
            MP40           = { SoundId = "rbxassetid://103807799095792" },
            Bow            = { SoundId = "rbxassetid://3442683707" },
        }

        local Config = {
            hit   = { enabled = false, name = "Gamesense", volume = 1, pitch = 1, minGap = 0.008 },
            shoot = { enabled = false, name = "AK47",      volume = 1, pitch = 1, minGap = 0.012 },
        }

        local fxFolder = Services.SoundService:FindFirstChild("arch_fx_sounds")
        if not fxFolder then
            fxFolder = Instance.new("Folder")
            fxFolder.Name   = "arch_fx_sounds"
            fxFolder.Parent = Services.SoundService
        end

        local live   = { hit = 0, shoot = 0 }
        local lastAt = { hit = 0, shoot = 0 }
        local MAX_LIVE = 8

        local function playFx(kind, soundId, volume, pitch, minGap)
            if type(soundId) ~= "string" or soundId == "" then return end
            local now = os.clock()
            if now - (lastAt[kind] or 0) < (minGap or 0.01) then return end
            lastAt[kind] = now
            if (live[kind] or 0) >= MAX_LIVE then return end

            local snd = Instance.new("Sound")
            snd.Name               = "arch_" .. kind
            snd.SoundId            = soundId
            snd.Volume             = math.clamp(volume or 1, 0, 5)
            snd.PlaybackSpeed      = math.clamp(pitch or 1, 0.1, 3)
            snd.RollOffMaxDistance = 10000
            snd.Parent             = fxFolder
            live[kind] = (live[kind] or 0) + 1

            local cleaned = false
            local function cleanup()
                if cleaned then return end
                cleaned = true
                live[kind] = math.max(0, (live[kind] or 1) - 1)
                pcall(function() snd:Destroy() end)
            end

            pcall(function() snd.Ended:Connect(cleanup) end)
            pcall(function() snd:Play() end)
            task.delay(6, cleanup)
        end

        local function playHit(force)
            if not force and not Config.hit.enabled then return end
            local entry = HIT_SOUNDS[Config.hit.name]
            if not entry then return end
            playFx("hit", entry.SoundId, Config.hit.volume, Config.hit.pitch, Config.hit.minGap)
        end

        local function playShoot(force)
            if not force and not Config.shoot.enabled then return end
            local entry = SHOOT_SOUNDS[Config.shoot.name]
            if not entry then return end
            playFx("shoot", entry.SoundId, Config.shoot.volume, Config.shoot.pitch, Config.shoot.minGap)
        end

        local installed = { hit = false, shoot = false }

        local function installHitHook()
            if installed.hit then return end
            local lp = Services.Players.LocalPlayer
            if not lp then return end
            local scripts = lp:FindFirstChild("PlayerScripts")
            if not scripts then return end

            local targetClass
            for _, path in {
                scripts.Modules.ClientReplicatedClasses.ClientFighter.ClientItem.ItemInterface.Mouse.MouseCrosshair,
                scripts.Modules.ClientReplicatedClasses.ClientFighter.ClientItem.ItemInterface,
            } do
                local ok, mod = pcall(require, path)
                if ok and type(mod) == "table" and type(mod.DamageEffect) == "function" then
                    targetClass = mod
                    break
                end
            end
            if not targetClass then return end

            local dmgKey   = utf8.char(0)
            local original = targetClass.DamageEffect
            targetClass.DamageEffect = function(self, payload, ...)
                if type(payload) == "table" then
                    local amount = payload[dmgKey]
                    if amount ~= nil and amount ~= 0 and amount ~= false then
                        pcall(playHit)
                    end
                end
                return original(self, payload, ...)
            end
            installed.hit = true
        end

        local function installShootHook()
            if installed.shoot then return end
            local lp = Services.Players.LocalPlayer
            if not lp then return end
            local ok, fc = pcall(require, lp.PlayerScripts.Controllers.FighterController)
            if not ok or type(fc) ~= "table" then return end

            local lastItem, conn
            local function bindItem(item)
                if conn then pcall(function() conn:Disconnect() end); conn = nil end
                lastItem = item
                if type(item) ~= "table" then return end
                local shot = item.Shot
                local sig = (type(shot) == "table" and type(shot.Connect) == "function")
                    or (typeof(shot) == "RBXScriptSignal")
                if not sig then return end
                local okC, c = pcall(function()
                    return shot:Connect(function()
                        pcall(playShoot)
                    end)
                end)
                if okC then conn = c end
            end

            task.spawn(function()
                while true do
                    local fighter = fc.LocalFighter
                    local item = fighter and (fighter.EquippedItem
                        or (type(fighter.Get) == "function" and fighter:Get("EquippedItem")))
                    if item ~= lastItem then bindItem(item) end
                    task.wait(0.5)
                end
            end)
            installed.shoot = true
        end

        local Sounds = {}

        function Sounds.start(run)
            run:spawn(function()
                for _ = 1, 60 do
                    installHitHook()
                    if installed.hit then break end
                    task.wait(0.5)
                end
            end)
            run:spawn(function()
                for _ = 1, 10 do
                    installShootHook()
                    if installed.shoot then break end
                    task.wait(0.5)
                end
            end)
        end

        Sounds.Config       = Config
        Sounds.HIT_SOUNDS   = HIT_SOUNDS
        Sounds.SHOOT_SOUNDS = SHOOT_SOUNDS
        Sounds.playHit      = playHit
        Sounds.playShoot    = playShoot

        return table.freeze(Sounds)
    end

    function __ANTIBRAIN_MODULES.bz()
        local v = __ANTIBRAIN_MODULES.cache.bz
        if not v then
            v = { c = __modImpl() }
            __ANTIBRAIN_MODULES.cache.bz = v
        end
        return v.c
    end
end

        local Aimbot = __ANTIBRAIN_MODULES.v()
            local AlwaysBackstab = __ANTIBRAIN_MODULES.z()

local SkyLighting  = game:GetService('Lighting')

local function setSkyProp(inst, prop, value)
    local bag = skyUndo.props[inst]
    if not bag then bag = {}; skyUndo.props[inst] = bag end
    local ok, cur = pcall(function() return (inst :: any)[prop] end)
    if not ok then return end
    if bag[prop] == nil then bag[prop] = cur end
    pcall(function() (inst :: any)[prop] = value end)
end

local function ensureSkyEffect(class, name)
    local held = SkyLighting:FindFirstChild(name)
    if held and not held:IsA(class) then held:Destroy(); held = nil end
    if not held then
        held = Instance.new(class); held.Name = name; held.Parent = SkyLighting
        skyUndo.effects[name] = held
    end
    return held
end

local function restoreStashedSkies()
    for _, sky in skyUndo.stashedSkies do
        pcall(function() if sky and sky.Parent == nil then sky.Parent = SkyLighting end end)
    end
    table.clear(skyUndo.stashedSkies)
end

local function stashOtherSkies()
    for _, child in SkyLighting:GetChildren() do
        if child:IsA('Sky') and child.Name ~= 'sonata_skybox' then
            table.insert(skyUndo.stashedSkies, child)
            pcall(function() child.Parent = nil end)
        end
    end
end

local function skyAssetId(value)
    if type(value) == 'string' then
        if string.find(value, 'rbxasset', 1, true) or string.find(value, 'http', 1, true) then return value end
        return 'rbxassetid://' .. value
    end
    return 'rbxassetid://' .. tostring(value)
end
