local SKYBOX_NAMES = {}
for name in SKYBOX_CATALOG do table.insert(SKYBOX_NAMES, name) end
table.sort(SKYBOX_NAMES)

local function applySkyboxOverride()
    local ours = SkyLighting:FindFirstChild('sonata_skybox')
    if not WorldSky.skybox then
        if ours then pcall(function() ours:Destroy() end) end
        restoreStashedSkies()
        if skyboxWatchConn then pcall(function() skyboxWatchConn:Disconnect() end); skyboxWatchConn = nil end
        return
    end
    local entry = SKYBOX_CATALOG[WorldSky.skyboxName]
    if not entry then
        WorldSky.skyboxName = SKYBOX_NAMES[1] or 'Afternoon'
        entry = SKYBOX_CATALOG[WorldSky.skyboxName]
    end
    if not entry then return end
    stashOtherSkies()
    local sky = ours
    if not sky or not sky:IsA('Sky') then
        sky = Instance.new('Sky'); sky.Name = 'sonata_skybox'; sky.Parent = SkyLighting
    end
    sky.SkyboxBk = skyAssetId(entry.SkyboxBk)
    sky.SkyboxDn = skyAssetId(entry.SkyboxDn)
    sky.SkyboxFt = skyAssetId(entry.SkyboxFt)
    sky.SkyboxLf = skyAssetId(entry.SkyboxLf)
    sky.SkyboxRt = skyAssetId(entry.SkyboxRt)
    sky.SkyboxUp = skyAssetId(entry.SkyboxUp)
    sky.StarCount = tonumber(entry.StarCount) or 3000
    if tonumber(entry.SunAngularSize) then sky.SunAngularSize = entry.SunAngularSize end
    if tonumber(entry.MoonAngularSize) then sky.MoonAngularSize = entry.MoonAngularSize end
    if type(entry.SunTextureId) == 'string' then sky.SunTextureId = entry.SunTextureId end
    if type(entry.MoonTextureId) == 'string' then sky.MoonTextureId = entry.MoonTextureId end
    sky.CelestialBodiesShown = (sky.SunAngularSize or 21) > 0 or (sky.MoonAngularSize or 11) > 0
    if not skyboxWatchConn then
        skyboxWatchConn = SkyLighting.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
            if WorldSky.skybox and child:IsA('Sky') and child.Name ~= 'sonata_skybox' then
                table.insert(skyUndo.stashedSkies, child)
                task.defer(function() pcall(function() child.Parent = nil end) end)
            end
        end)
        context.scope:add(function() pcall(function() skyboxWatchConn:Disconnect() end) end)
    end
end

local function applyWorldSky()
    if WorldSky.clock then setSkyProp(SkyLighting, 'ClockTime', WorldSky.clockTime) end
    if WorldSky.ambience then
        setSkyProp(SkyLighting, 'Ambient', WorldSky.ambient)
        setSkyProp(SkyLighting, 'OutdoorAmbient', WorldSky.outdoorAmbient)
    end
    if WorldSky.brightness then setSkyProp(SkyLighting, 'Brightness', WorldSky.brightnessVal) end
    if WorldSky.exposure then setSkyProp(SkyLighting, 'ExposureCompensation', WorldSky.exposureVal) end
    if WorldSky.fog then
        setSkyProp(SkyLighting, 'FogColor', WorldSky.fogColor)
        setSkyProp(SkyLighting, 'FogStart', WorldSky.fogStart)
        setSkyProp(SkyLighting, 'FogEnd', WorldSky.fogEnd)
    end
    if WorldSky.shadows then setSkyProp(SkyLighting, 'GlobalShadows', WorldSky.shadowsOn == true) end
    if WorldSky.envDiffuse then setSkyProp(SkyLighting, 'EnvironmentDiffuseScale', math.clamp(WorldSky.envDiffuseVal, 0, 3)) end
    if WorldSky.atmosphere then
        local atm = SkyLighting:FindFirstChildOfClass('Atmosphere')
        if not atm then
            atm = Instance.new('Atmosphere'); atm.Parent = SkyLighting
            skyUndo.effects.__atm = atm
        end
        setSkyProp(atm, 'Density', WorldSky.atmDensity)
        setSkyProp(atm, 'Offset', WorldSky.atmOffset)
        setSkyProp(atm, 'Color', WorldSky.atmColor)
        setSkyProp(atm, 'Decay', WorldSky.atmDecay)
        setSkyProp(atm, 'Glare', WorldSky.atmGlare)
        setSkyProp(atm, 'Haze', WorldSky.atmHaze)
    end
    if WorldSky.colorCorrection then
        local cc = ensureSkyEffect('ColorCorrectionEffect', 'sonata_cc')
        cc.Brightness = WorldSky.ccBrightness
        cc.Contrast   = WorldSky.ccContrast
        cc.Saturation = WorldSky.ccSaturation
        cc.TintColor  = WorldSky.ccTint
        cc.Enabled    = true
    else
        local cc = SkyLighting:FindFirstChild('sonata_cc'); if cc then cc.Enabled = false end
    end
    if WorldSky.bloom then
        local b = ensureSkyEffect('BloomEffect', 'sonata_bloom')
        b.Intensity = WorldSky.bloomIntensity
        b.Size      = WorldSky.bloomSize
        b.Threshold = WorldSky.bloomThreshold
        b.Enabled   = true
    else
        local b = SkyLighting:FindFirstChild('sonata_bloom'); if b then b.Enabled = false end
    end
    if WorldFx.blur then
        local b = ensureSkyEffect('BlurEffect', 'sonata_blur')
        b.Size = math.clamp(WorldFx.blurSize, 0, 56); b.Enabled = true
    else
        local b = SkyLighting:FindFirstChild('sonata_blur'); if b then b.Enabled = false end
    end
    if WorldFx.dof then
        local d = ensureSkyEffect('DepthOfFieldEffect', 'sonata_dof')
        d.FarIntensity  = math.clamp(WorldFx.dofFar, 0, 1)
        d.FocusDistance = math.clamp(WorldFx.dofFocus, 1, 200)
        d.InFocusRadius = math.clamp(WorldFx.dofIn, 0, 100)
        d.NearIntensity = math.clamp(WorldFx.dofNear, 0, 1)
        d.Enabled       = true
    else
        local d = SkyLighting:FindFirstChild('sonata_dof'); if d then d.Enabled = false end
    end
    if WorldFx.sunRays then
        local s = ensureSkyEffect('SunRaysEffect', 'sonata_sunrays')
        s.Intensity = math.clamp(WorldFx.sunIntensity, 0, 1)
        s.Spread    = math.clamp(WorldFx.sunSpread, 0, 1)
        s.Enabled   = true
    else
        local s = SkyLighting:FindFirstChild('sonata_sunrays'); if s then s.Enabled = false end
    end
    applySkyboxOverride()
end

local function applyTheme(name)
    WorldSky.theme = name
    WorldSky.clock, WorldSky.ambience, WorldSky.atmosphere = true, true, true
    WorldSky.brightness, WorldSky.exposure, WorldSky.fog = true, true, true
    WorldSky.colorCorrection = true
    WorldSky.bloom = (name ~= 'Light')
    WorldSky.shadows, WorldSky.envDiffuse = true, true
    if name == 'Dark' then
        WorldSky.clockTime = 0
        WorldSky.ambient = Color3.fromRGB(18,18,22); WorldSky.outdoorAmbient = Color3.fromRGB(10,10,14)
        WorldSky.atmDensity = 0.28; WorldSky.atmOffset = 0.05
        WorldSky.atmColor = Color3.fromRGB(40,40,48); WorldSky.atmDecay = Color3.fromRGB(12,12,16)
        WorldSky.atmGlare = 0; WorldSky.atmHaze = 0.15
        WorldSky.brightnessVal = 0.35; WorldSky.exposureVal = -0.4
        WorldSky.fogColor = Color3.fromRGB(8,8,12); WorldSky.fogStart = 0; WorldSky.fogEnd = 1800
        WorldSky.ccBrightness = -0.06; WorldSky.ccContrast = 0.12; WorldSky.ccSaturation = -0.08
        WorldSky.ccTint = Color3.fromRGB(210,210,230)
        WorldSky.bloomIntensity = 0.4; WorldSky.bloomSize = 18; WorldSky.bloomThreshold = 1.4
        WorldSky.envDiffuseVal = 0.25
    elseif name == 'Light' then
        WorldSky.clockTime = 14
        WorldSky.ambient = Color3.fromRGB(200,200,205); WorldSky.outdoorAmbient = Color3.fromRGB(180,185,195)
        WorldSky.atmDensity = 0.12; WorldSky.atmOffset = 0.2
        WorldSky.atmColor = Color3.fromRGB(220,225,235); WorldSky.atmDecay = Color3.fromRGB(160,170,190)
        WorldSky.atmGlare = 0.1; WorldSky.atmHaze = 0
        WorldSky.brightnessVal = 2.4; WorldSky.exposureVal = 0.15
        WorldSky.fogColor = Color3.fromRGB(230,235,245); WorldSky.fogStart = 0; WorldSky.fogEnd = 100000
        WorldSky.ccBrightness = 0.04; WorldSky.ccContrast = 0.02; WorldSky.ccSaturation = 0.05
        WorldSky.ccTint = Color3.new(1,1,1)
        WorldSky.bloomIntensity = 0.2; WorldSky.bloomSize = 12; WorldSky.bloomThreshold = 2
        WorldSky.envDiffuseVal = 1
    elseif name == 'Sepia' then
        WorldSky.clockTime = 16.5
        WorldSky.ambient = Color3.fromRGB(90,70,45); WorldSky.outdoorAmbient = Color3.fromRGB(70,52,32)
        WorldSky.atmDensity = 0.32; WorldSky.atmOffset = 0.1
        WorldSky.atmColor = Color3.fromRGB(180,140,90); WorldSky.atmDecay = Color3.fromRGB(90,55,30)
        WorldSky.atmGlare = 0.25; WorldSky.atmHaze = 0.2
        WorldSky.brightnessVal = 1.4; WorldSky.exposureVal = 0.05
        WorldSky.fogColor = Color3.fromRGB(160,120,70); WorldSky.fogStart = 20; WorldSky.fogEnd = 2800
        WorldSky.ccBrightness = 0.02; WorldSky.ccContrast = 0.08; WorldSky.ccSaturation = -0.15
        WorldSky.ccTint = Color3.fromRGB(255,210,150)
        WorldSky.bloomIntensity = 0.55; WorldSky.bloomSize = 22; WorldSky.bloomThreshold = 1.2
        WorldSky.envDiffuseVal = 0.7
    else
        WorldSky.clockTime = 3.2
        WorldSky.ambient = Color3.fromRGB(12,16,32); WorldSky.outdoorAmbient = Color3.fromRGB(6,10,24)
        WorldSky.atmDensity = 0.38; WorldSky.atmOffset = 0
        WorldSky.atmColor = Color3.fromRGB(20,30,70); WorldSky.atmDecay = Color3.fromRGB(4,8,22)
        WorldSky.atmGlare = 0; WorldSky.atmHaze = 0.35
        WorldSky.brightnessVal = 0.25; WorldSky.exposureVal = -0.55
        WorldSky.fogColor = Color3.fromRGB(8,12,28); WorldSky.fogStart = 0; WorldSky.fogEnd = 1400
        WorldSky.ccBrightness = -0.1; WorldSky.ccContrast = 0.18; WorldSky.ccSaturation = -0.12
        WorldSky.ccTint = Color3.fromRGB(160,180,255)
        WorldSky.bloomIntensity = 0.7; WorldSky.bloomSize = 28; WorldSky.bloomThreshold = 1
        WorldSky.envDiffuseVal = 0.15
    end
    applyWorldSky()
end

local function revertSky()
    for _, name in {'sonata_cc','sonata_bloom','sonata_blur','sonata_dof','sonata_sunrays','sonata_skybox'} do
        local inst = SkyLighting:FindFirstChild(name)
        if inst then pcall(function() inst:Destroy() end) end
    end
    if skyUndo.effects.__atm then
        pcall(function() skyUndo.effects.__atm:Destroy() end)
        skyUndo.effects.__atm = nil
    end
    for inst, bag in skyUndo.props do
        for prop, value in bag do pcall(function() (inst :: any)[prop] = value end) end
    end
    table.clear(skyUndo.props)
    table.clear(skyUndo.effects)
    restoreStashedSkies()
end

        { Texture=SOFT_TEX, TransparencyMax=0.2, LifetimeMin=3, LifetimeMax=5, BaseRate=620,
          SpeedMin=16, SpeedMax=26, SizeStart=0.26, SizeEnd=0.18, BaseSpread=0.7, BaseAccelerationY=-5 },
        { Texture=SOFT_TEX, TransparencyMax=0.82, LifetimeMin=3, LifetimeMax=5, BaseRate=90,
          SpeedMin=12, SpeedMax=20, SizeStart=2.4, SizeEnd=4.2, BaseSpread=0.9, BaseAccelerationY=-1.6 },
    },
}
local weatherPart = nil
local weatherEmitters = {}
local lastWeatherType = ''

local function weatherKind()
    local kind = string.lower(tostring(WorldSky.weatherType or 'Snow'))
    if kind == 'rain' then return 'Rain' end
    if kind == 'blizzard' then return 'Blizzard' end
    return 'Snow'
end

local function destroyWeather()
    for _, emitter in weatherEmitters do pcall(function() emitter:Destroy() end) end
    table.clear(weatherEmitters)
    if weatherPart then pcall(function() weatherPart:Destroy() end); weatherPart = nil end
    lastWeatherType = ''
end

local function fadeSequence(peak)
    peak = math.clamp(peak, 0, 1)
    local mid = peak + (1 - peak) * 0.22
    return NumberSequence.new({
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 1),
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.1, mid),
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.22, peak),
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.78, peak),
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.9, mid),
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1),
    })
end

local function ensureWeather()
    local kind = weatherKind()
    if weatherPart and weatherPart.Parent and lastWeatherType == kind and #weatherEmitters > 0 then
        return weatherPart
    end
    destroyWeather()
    local part = Instance.new('Part')
    part.Name = 'sonata_weather'; part.Anchored = true
    part.CanCollide, part.CanQuery, part.CanTouch = false, false, false
    part.Transparency = 1; part.Size = Vector3.new(148, 2, 148)
    part.CastShadow, part.Massless = false, true
    part.Parent = SkyWorkspace
    for _, spec in (KICIA_WEATHER[kind] or KICIA_WEATHER.Snow) do
        local emitter = Instance.new('ParticleEmitter')
        emitter.Name = 'Weather'
        emitter.Shape = Enum.ParticleEmitterShape.Box
        emitter.EmissionDirection = Enum.NormalId.Bottom
        emitter.Texture = spec.Texture
        emitter.Transparency = fadeSequence(spec.TransparencyMax)
        emitter.Lifetime = NumberRange.new(spec.LifetimeMin, spec.LifetimeMax)
        emitter.Rotation = NumberRange.new(spec.RotationMin or 0, spec.RotationMax or 360)
        emitter.RotSpeed = NumberRange.new(spec.RotationSpeedMin or -40, spec.RotationSpeedMax or 40)
        emitter.LightInfluence = 0; emitter.LockedToPart = false; emitter.ZOffset = 0
        if spec.Orientation then emitter.Orientation = spec.Orientation end
        emitter.Parent = part
        table.insert(weatherEmitters, emitter)
    end
    weatherPart = part
    lastWeatherType = kind
    return part
end

local function paintWeather(part)
    local kind = weatherKind()
    local specs = KICIA_WEATHER[kind] or KICIA_WEATHER.Snow
    local intensity = math.clamp((tonumber(WorldSky.weatherIntensity) or 100) / 100, 0, 3)
    local rate      = math.clamp((tonumber(WorldSky.weatherAmount) or 100) / 100, 0, 4)
    local speed     = math.clamp(tonumber(WorldSky.weatherSpeed) or 1, 0.05, 8)
    local size      = math.clamp(tonumber(WorldSky.weatherSize) or 1, 0.05, 6)
    local glow      = math.clamp(tonumber(WorldSky.weatherGlow) or 0, 0, 1)
    local spread    = math.clamp(tonumber(WorldSky.weatherSpread) or 1, 0, 4)
    local opacity   = math.clamp((tonumber(WorldSky.weatherOpacity) or 85) / 100, 0, 1)
    local wind      = math.clamp(tonumber(WorldSky.weatherWind) or 6, 0, 80)
    local rad       = math.rad(math.clamp(tonumber(WorldSky.weatherDirection) or 20, 0, 360))
    local tint      = if typeof(WorldSky.weatherColor) == 'Color3' then WorldSky.weatherColor else Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255)
    local color     = ColorSequence.new(tint)
    part.Size = Vector3.new(148, 2, 148)
    for i, emitter in weatherEmitters do
        local spec = specs[i] or specs[1]
        local peak = 1 - (1 - (spec.TransparencyMax or 0.2)) * opacity
        emitter.Rate    = spec.BaseRate * intensity * rate
        emitter.Speed   = NumberRange.new(spec.SpeedMin * speed, spec.SpeedMax * speed)
        emitter.Size    = NumberSequence.new(spec.SizeStart * size, spec.SizeEnd * size)
        emitter.LightEmission = glow
        emitter.Transparency  = fadeSequence(peak)
        emitter.Drag = if kind == 'Rain' then 0 else 1.15
        local ang = spec.BaseSpread * spread
        emitter.SpreadAngle = Vector2.new(ang, ang)
        emitter.Color = color
        emitter.Acceleration = Vector3.new(math.sin(rad) * wind, spec.BaseAccelerationY, math.cos(rad) * wind)
        emitter.Enabled = opacity > 0.01 and intensity > 0 and rate > 0
    end
end

local lastWeatherSig = ''
local function syncWeather()
    if not WorldSky.weather then
        if weatherPart then destroyWeather() end
        lastWeatherSig = ''
        return
    end
    local part = ensureWeather()
    if not part then return end
    local cam = SkyWorkspace.CurrentCamera
    if not cam then return end
    local height = math.clamp(tonumber(WorldSky.weatherHeight) or 40, 8, 160)
    part.CFrame = CFrame.new(cam.CFrame.Position + Vector3.new(0, height, 0))
    local sig = table.concat({
        weatherKind(),
        string.format('%.2f', WorldSky.weatherIntensity),
        string.format('%.2f', WorldSky.weatherAmount),
        string.format('%.2f', WorldSky.weatherHeight),
        string.format('%.2f', WorldSky.weatherSize),
        string.format('%.2f', WorldSky.weatherSpeed),
        string.format('%.2f', WorldSky.weatherGlow),
        string.format('%.2f', WorldSky.weatherSpread),
        string.format('%.2f', WorldSky.weatherOpacity),
        string.format('%.2f', WorldSky.weatherWind),
        string.format('%.0f', WorldSky.weatherDirection),
        tostring(WorldSky.weatherColor),
    }, '|')
    if sig ~= lastWeatherSig then
        lastWeatherSig = sig
        paintWeather(part)
    end
end

-- 天气跟随相机 + 光照低频重刷（防游戏覆盖）
context.scope:add(SkyRun.Heartbeat:Connect(function() pcall(syncWeather) end))
context.scope:spawn(function()
    while not context.scope.closed do
        task.wait(1)
        local active = WorldSky.clock or WorldSky.ambience or WorldSky.atmosphere
            or WorldSky.brightness or WorldSky.exposure or WorldSky.fog
            or WorldSky.colorCorrection or WorldSky.bloom or WorldSky.skybox
            or WorldFx.blur or WorldFx.dof or WorldFx.sunRays
        if active then pcall(applyWorldSky) end
    end
end)

-- ▲▲▲ 完整光照分页

local LightingSvc = game:GetService('Lighting')
local postUndo = { props = setmetatable({}, {__mode = 'k'}), effects = {} }

local function setProp(undo, inst, prop, value)
    local bag = undo.props[inst] or {}
    if bag[prop] == nil then
        local ok, cur = pcall(function() return (inst :: any)[prop] end)
        if ok then bag[prop] = cur end
    end
    undo.props[inst] = bag
    pcall(function() (inst :: any)[prop] = value end)
end

local function ensureEffect(undo, class, name)
    local held = LightingSvc:FindFirstChild(name)
    if held and not held:IsA(class) then held:Destroy(); held = nil end
    if not held then
        held = Instance.new(class)
        held.Name = name
        held.Parent = LightingSvc
        undo.effects[name] = held
    end
    return held
end

local function revertUndo(undo)
    for inst, bag in undo.props do
        for prop, value in bag do pcall(function() (inst :: any)[prop] = value end) end
    end
    table.clear(undo.props)
    for _, inst in undo.effects do pcall(function() (inst :: any):Destroy() end) end
    table.clear(undo.effects)
end
-- ▲▲▲ 光照 / 后期 UI

-- ▼▼▼ 后期控件
local ccToggle = postPage:toggle(context.ui.id('extras-post-cc'), {
    text = 'color correction', default = false,
})
local bloomSlider = postPage:slider(context.ui.id('extras-post-bloom'), {
    text = 'bloom', default = 0, min = 0, max = 100, rounding = 0, suffix = '%',
})
local blurSlider = postPage:slider(context.ui.id('extras-post-blur'), {
    text = 'blur', default = 0, min = 0, max = 56, rounding = 0,
})
local dofToggle = postPage:toggle(context.ui.id('extras-post-dof'), {
    text = 'depth of field', default = false,
})
local raysToggle = postPage:toggle(context.ui.id('extras-post-sunrays'), {
    text = 'sun rays', default = false,
})
-- ▲▲▲ 后期控件
-- ▼▼▼ 后期 sync
local function syncPost()
    revertUndo(postUndo)
    if ccToggle.value then
        local cc = ensureEffect(postUndo, 'ColorCorrectionEffect', '__arch_cc')
        pcall(function() cc.Enabled = true end)
    end
    if bloomSlider.value > 0 then
        local b = ensureEffect(postUndo, 'BloomEffect', '__arch_bloom')
        pcall(function() b.Intensity = bloomSlider.value / 100; b.Enabled = true end)
    end
    if blurSlider.value > 0 then
        local b = ensureEffect(postUndo, 'BlurEffect', '__arch_blur')
        pcall(function() b.Size = blurSlider.value; b.Enabled = true end)
    end
    if dofToggle.value then
        pcall(function() ensureEffect(postUndo, 'DepthOfFieldEffect', '__arch_dof').Enabled = true end)
    end
    if raysToggle.value then
        pcall(function() ensureEffect(postUndo, 'SunRaysEffect', '__arch_sunrays').Enabled = true end)
    end
end
ccToggle:onChanged(syncPost)
bloomSlider:onChanged(syncPost)
blurSlider:onChanged(syncPost)
dofToggle:onChanged(syncPost)
raysToggle:onChanged(syncPost)
context.scope:add(function() revertUndo(postUndo) end)

-- Lua & Servers
local LuaTools = __ANTIBRAIN_MODULES.by()

local scriptList = luaPage:dropdown(context.ui.id('extras-lua-script'), {
    text = 'script',
    values = {},
    default = nil,
    allowNull = true,
})

local function refreshScripts()
    local scripts = LuaTools.listScripts()
    local names = {}
    for _, s in scripts do table.insert(names, s.name) end
    if #names == 0 then names = { "none" } end
    scriptList:setValues(names)
end

luaPage:button({
    text = 'load',
    callback = function()
        local name = scriptList.value
        if not name or name == 'none' then return end
        for _, s in LuaTools.listScripts() do
            if s.name == name then
                LuaTools.runScript(s.name, s.path)
                return
            end
        end
    end,
})
luaPage:button({
    text = 'refresh',
    callback = refreshScripts,
})
luaPage:button({
    text = 'delete',
    doubleClick = true,
    callback = function()
        local name = scriptList.value
        if not name or name == 'none' then return end
        for _, s in LuaTools.listScripts() do
            if s.name == name then
                LuaTools.deleteScript(s.name, s.path)
                refreshScripts()
                return
            end
        end
    end,
})

refreshScripts()

luaPage:divider()

local serverList = luaPage:dropdown(context.ui.id('extras-lua-server'), {
    text = 'server',
    values = {},
    default = nil,
    allowNull = true,
})

local serverRows = {}

local function refreshServers()
    local rows = LuaTools.listServers()
    local names = {}
    serverRows = {}
    for _, r in rows do
        local label = string.format(
            "%d/%d%s",
            r.playing, r.maxPlayers,
            r.ping and (" · " .. math.floor(r.ping) .. "ms") or "")
        table.insert(names, label)
        serverRows[label] = r
    end
    if #names == 0 then names = { "none" } end
    serverList:setValues(names)
end

luaPage:button({
    text = 'refresh servers',
    callback = refreshServers,
})
luaPage:button({
    text = 'join server',
    callback = function()
        local label = serverList.value
        if not label or label == 'none' then return end
        local row = serverRows[label]
        if row then LuaTools.joinServer(row.id) end
    end,
})

        function __ANTIBRAIN_MODULES.ap()

    do
        local function __modImpl()
            __ANTIBRAIN_MODULES.g()

            return table.freeze({
                games = table.freeze({
                    (__ANTIBRAIN_MODULES.ap()),
                }),
                universal = nil,
            })
        end

        function __ANTIBRAIN_MODULES.aq()
            local v = __ANTIBRAIN_MODULES.cache.aq

            if not v then
                v = {
                    c = __modImpl(),
                }
                __ANTIBRAIN_MODULES.cache.aq = v
            end

            return v.c
        end
    end
    do
        local function __modImpl()
            local Failure = __ANTIBRAIN_MODULES.a()
            local Services = __ANTIBRAIN_MODULES.k()
            local Files = {}

            function Files.validName(name)
                if not name:match('^[%w _%-][%w _%-%.]*$') or #name > 64 or name:match('[%. ]$') then
                    return false
                end

                local stem = (name:match('^[^%.]+') or name):upper()

                return stem ~= 'CON' and stem ~= 'PRN' and stem ~= 'AUX' and stem ~= 'NUL' and not stem:match('^COM[1-9]$') and not stem:match('^LPT[1-9]$')
            end

            local function ensure(folder)
                local path = ''

                for part in folder:gmatch('[^/]+')do
                    path = (path == '' and {part} or {
                        string.format('%s/%s', tostring(path), tostring(part)),
                    })[1]

                    if not isfolder(path) then
                        makefolder(path)
                    end
                end
            end

            function Files.prepare(folders)
                local failed = nil

                for _, folder in folders do
                    local failure = Failure.capture(ensure, folder)

                    if failure and not failed then
                        failed = string.format('could not make %s: %s', tostring(folder), tostring(Failure.summary(failure)))
                    end
                end

                return failed
            end
            function Files.seed(path, value)
                local ok, present = pcall(function()
                    return isfile(path)
                end)

                if not ok then
                    return string.format('could not look for %s: %s', tostring(path), tostring(present))
                end
                if present then
                    return nil
                end

                return Files.write(path, value)
            end
            function Files.read(path)
                local ok, result = pcall(function()
                    if not isfile(path) then
                        return nil
                    end

                    local value = Services.HttpService:JSONDecode(readfile(path))

                    assert(value ~= nil, 'the file contains JSON null')

                    return value
                end)

                if not ok then
                    return nil, string.format('could not read %s: %s', tostring(path), tostring(result))
                end

                return result, nil
            end
            function Files.readRaw(path)
                local ok, result = pcall(function()
                    if not isfile(path) then
                        return nil
                    end

                    return readfile(path)
                end)

                if not ok then
                    return nil, string.format('could not read %s: %s', tostring(path), tostring(result))
                end

                return result, nil
            end
            function Files.writeRaw(path, text)
                local ok, failure = pcall(function()
                    ensure(path:match('^(.*)/[^/]*$') or '')
                    writefile(path, text)
                end)

                return (ok and {nil} or {
                    string.format('could not write %s: %s', tostring(path), tostring(failure)),
                })[1]
            end
            function Files.write(path, value)
                local ok, failure = pcall(function()
                    ensure(path:match('^(.*)/[^/]*$') or '')
                    writefile(path, Services.HttpService:JSONEncode(value))
                end)

                return (ok and {nil} or {
                    string.format('could not write %s: %s', tostring(path), tostring(failure)),
                })[1]
            end
            function Files.delete(path)
                local ok, failure = pcall(function()
                    if isfile(path) then
                        delfile(path)
                    end
                end)

                return (ok and {nil} or {
                    string.format('could not delete %s: %s', tostring(path), tostring(failure)),
                })[1]
            end
            function Files.entries(folder)
                local ok, paths = pcall(function()
                    if not isfolder(folder) then
                        return {}
                    end

                    return listfiles(folder)
                end)

                if not ok then
                    return {}, string.format('could not list %s: %s', tostring(folder), tostring(paths))
                end

                local names = {}

                for _, path in paths do
                    local name = path:match('([^/\\]+)$')

                    if name then
                        table.insert(names, name)
                    end
                end

                table.sort(names)

                return names, nil
            end
            function Files.names(folder)
                local names = {}

                for _, entry in Files.entries(folder)do
                    local name = entry:match('^(.+)%.json$')

                    if name and Files.validName(name) then
                        table.insert(names, name)
                    end
                end

                return names
            end
            function Files.isFile(path)
                local ok, found = pcall(function()
                    return isfile(path)
                end)

                return ok and found
            end
            function Files.isFolder(path)
                local ok, found = pcall(function()
                    return isfolder(path)
                end)

                if not ok then
                    return false, string.format('could not inspect %s: %s', tostring(path), tostring(found))
                end

                return found, nil
            end
            function Files.removeEmpty(path)
                local ok = pcall(function()
                    local entries, failure = Files.entries(path)

                    if not failure and #entries == 0 then
                        (delfolder)(path)
                    end
                end)

                return ok and not Files.isFolder(path)
            end

            return table.freeze(Files)
        end

        function __ANTIBRAIN_MODULES.ar()
            local v = __ANTIBRAIN_MODULES.cache.ar

            if not v then
                v = {
                    c = __modImpl(),
                }
                __ANTIBRAIN_MODULES.cache.ar = v
            end

            return v.c
        end
    end
    do
        local function __modImpl()
            return table.freeze({
                name = 'Arch Script Premium',
                version = '0.1.0',
                folder = 'Arch script ',
            })
        end

        function __ANTIBRAIN_MODULES.as()
            local v = __ANTIBRAIN_MODULES.cache.as

            if not v then
                v = {
                    c = __modImpl(),
                }
                __ANTIBRAIN_MODULES.cache.as = v
            end

            return v.c
        end
    end
    do
        local function __modImpl()
            local Meta = __ANTIBRAIN_MODULES.as()
            local Paths = {}

            Paths.LAYOUT = 2

            function Paths.root()
                return Meta.folder
            end
            function Paths.settings()
                return string.format('%s/settings.json', tostring(Meta.folder))
            end
            function Paths.key(scriptId)
                assert(scriptId:match('^[a-f0-9]+$'), 'Invalid script ID for a saved key path')

                return string.format('%s/keys/%s.txt', tostring(Meta.folder), tostring(scriptId))
            end
            function Paths.themes()
                return string.format('%s/themes', tostring(Meta.folder))
            end
            function Paths.theme(name)
                return string.format('%s/%s.json', tostring(Paths.themes()), tostring(name))
            end
            function Paths.games()
                return string.format('%s/games', tostring(Meta.folder))
            end
            function Paths.configs(game)
                return string.format('%s/%s/configs', tostring(Paths.games()), tostring(game))
            end
            function Paths.config(game, name)
                return string.format('%s/%s.json', tostring(Paths.configs(game)), tostring(name))
            end
            function Paths.folders(game)
                local folders = {
                    Meta.folder,
                    Paths.themes(),
                    Paths.games(),
                }

                if game then
                    table.insert(folders, string.format('%s/%s', tostring(Paths.games()), tostring(game)))
                    table.insert(folders, Paths.configs(game))
                end

                return folders
            end

            return table.freeze(Paths)
        end

        function __ANTIBRAIN_MODULES.at()
            local v = __ANTIBRAIN_MODULES.cache.at

            if not v then
                v = {
                    c = __modImpl(),
                }
                __ANTIBRAIN_MODULES.cache.at = v
            end

            return v.c
        end
    end
    do
        local function __modImpl()
            local Failure = __ANTIBRAIN_MODULES.a()
            local Files = __ANTIBRAIN_MODULES.ar()
            local Paths = __ANTIBRAIN_MODULES.at()
            local Migration = {}
            local NOT_GAMES = table.freeze({
                themes = true,
                games = true,
            })

            local function move(game, name, oldFolder)
                local from, to = string.format('%s/%s', tostring(oldFolder), tostring(name)), Paths.config(game, (name:gsub('%.json$', '')))
                local text, readFailure = Files.readRaw(from)

                if not text then
                    return readFailure
                end

                local existing, targetFailure = Files.readRaw(to)

                if targetFailure then
                    return targetFailure
                end
                if existing ~= nil then
                    if existing == text then
                        return Files.delete(from)
                    end

                    return string.format(
[[the saved config at %s differs from %s; both files were kept]], tostring(to), tostring(from))
                end

                local failure = Files.writeRaw(to, text)

                if failure then
                    return failure
                end
                if Files.readRaw(to) ~= text then
                    return string.format('the copy of %s does not match it', tostring(from))
                end

                return Files.delete(from)
            end
            local function moveGame(game, failures)
                local oldFolder = string.format('%s/%s/configs', tostring(Paths.root()), tostring(game))
                local entries, listingFailure = Files.entries(oldFolder)

                if listingFailure then
                    table.insert(failures, listingFailure)

                    return
                end

                for _, name in entries do
                    if name:match('%.json$') then
                        local failure = move(game, name, oldFolder)

                        if failure then
                            table.insert(failures, failure)
                        end
                    end
                end

                if Files.removeEmpty(oldFolder) then
                    Files.removeEmpty(string.format('%s/%s', tostring(Paths.root()), tostring(game)))
                end
            end

            function Migration.version(settings)
                return (type(settings) == 'table' and type(settings.version) == 'number' and {
                    settings.version,
                } or {1})[1]
            end
            function Migration.run()
                local settings, readFailure = Files.read(Paths.settings())

                if readFailure then
                    return readFailure
                end
                if settings ~= nil and type(settings) ~= 'table' then
                    return 
[[Saved settings are not a JSON object; migration was not started]]
                end
                if type(settings) == 'table' and settings.version ~= nil then
                    local version = settings.version

                    if type(version) ~= 'number' or version < 1 or version % 1 ~= 0 then
                        return 
[[Saved settings have an invalid layout version; migration was not started]]
                    end
                end
                if Migration.version(settings) >= Paths.LAYOUT then
                    return nil
                end

                local failures = {}
                local failure = Failure.capture(function()
                    local entries, listingFailure = Files.entries(Paths.root())

                    if listingFailure then
                        table.insert(failures, listingFailure)

                        return
                    end

                    for _, game in entries do
                        if not NOT_GAMES[game] then
                            local present, folderFailure = Files.isFolder(string.format('%s/%s/configs', tostring(Paths.root()), tostring(game)))

                            if folderFailure then
                                table.insert(failures, folderFailure)
                            elseif present then
                                moveGame(game, failures)
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end)

                if failure then
                    table.insert(failures, Failure.summary(failure))
                end
                if #failures > 0 then
                    return string.format('some saved files could not be moved to the new layout: %s', tostring(table.concat(failures, '; ')))
                end
                if type(settings) == 'table' then
                    settings.version = Paths.LAYOUT

                    return Files.write(Paths.settings(), settings)
                end

                return nil
            end

            return table.freeze(Migration)
        end

        function __ANTIBRAIN_MODULES.au()
            local v = __ANTIBRAIN_MODULES.cache.au

            if not v then
                v = {
                    c = __modImpl(),
                }
                __ANTIBRAIN_MODULES.cache.au = v
            end

            return v.c
        end
    end
    do
        local function __modImpl()
            local Files = __ANTIBRAIN_MODULES.ar()
            local Meta = __ANTIBRAIN_MODULES.as()
            local Migration = __ANTIBRAIN_MODULES.au()
            local Paths = __ANTIBRAIN_MODULES.at()

            __ANTIBRAIN_MODULES.f()

            local Services = __ANTIBRAIN_MODULES.k()
            local REQUIRED = table.freeze({
                'getgenv',
                'gethui',
            })
            local FILE_APIS = table.freeze({
                'readfile',
                'writefile',
                'isfile',
                'isfolder',
                'makefolder',
                'listfiles',
                'delfile',
            })
            local SLOT_KEY = '__ANTIBRAIN_SESSION_V1'

            local function claimSlot(environment)
                if environment[SLOT_KEY] == nil then
                    environment[SLOT_KEY] = {}
                end

                local slot = environment[SLOT_KEY]

                assert(type(slot) == 'table', string.format('The %s session slot holds an incompatible value', tostring(Meta.name)))
                assert(slot.current == nil or (type(slot.current) == 'table' and type(slot.current.stop) == 'function'), string.format('An incompatible %s session is running; unload it first', tostring(Meta.name)))

                return slot
            end
            local function executorName(environment)
                if type(environment.identifyexecutor) ~= 'function' then
                    return 'Unknown executor'
                end

                local ok, name, version = pcall(environment.identifyexecutor)

                if not ok or type(name) ~= 'string' then
                    return 'Unknown executor'
                end

                return (type(version) == 'string' and version ~= '' and {
                    string.format('%s %s', tostring(name), tostring(version)),
                } or {name})[1]
            end
            local function experienceName()
                local ok, info = pcall(function()
                    return Services.MarketplaceService:GetProductInfoAsync(game.PlaceId)
                end)

                return (ok and type(info) == 'table' and type(info.Name) == 'string' and {
                    info.Name,
                } or {
                    game.Name,
                })[1]
            end

            local Executor = {}

            function Executor.inspect(environment)
                local missing = {}

                for _, name in REQUIRED do
                    if type(environment[name]) ~= 'function' then
                        table.insert(missing, name)
                    end
                end

                local persistence = true

                for _, name in FILE_APIS do
                    if type(environment[name]) ~= 'function' then
                        persistence = false

                        break
                    end
                end

                return {
                    missing = missing,
                    persistence = persistence,
                }
            end
            function Executor.supports(environment, name)
                local value = environment

                for part in (string.format('%s.', tostring(name))):gmatch('([^.]*)%.')do
                    if type(value) ~= 'table' or not part:match('^[%a_][%w_]*$') then
                        return false
                    end

                    value = value[part]
                end

                return type(value) == 'function' or type(value) == 'table'
            end
            function Executor.connect()
                assert(type(getgenv) == 'function', string.format('%s requires an executor with getgenv', tostring(Meta.name)))

                local environment = getgenv()
                local capabilities = Executor.inspect(environment)

                assert(#capabilities.missing == 0, string.format('Missing executor APIs: %s', tostring(table.concat(capabilities.missing, ', '))))

                if not game:IsLoaded() then
                    game.Loaded:Wait()
                end

                local function log(message)
                    warn(string.format('[%s] %s', tostring(Meta.name), tostring(message)))
                end

                local info = {
                    placeId = game.PlaceId,
                    universeId = game.GameId,
                    name = game.Name,
                }

                return {
                    slot = claimSlot(environment),
                    game = info,
                    executor = executorName(environment),
                    persistence = capabilities.persistence,
                    initialize = function(scope)
                        scope:spawn(function()
                            info.name = experienceName()
                        end)
                    end,
                    supports = function(name)
                        return Executor.supports(environment, name)
                    end,
                    prepare = function(module)
                        if not capabilities.persistence then
                            return nil
                        end

                        local failure = Files.prepare(Paths.folders(module))

                        if module == nil then
                            failure = failure or Migration.run()
                        end

                        return failure or Files.seed(Paths.settings(), {
                            version = Paths.LAYOUT,
                        })
                    end,
                    wait = function(seconds)
                        task.wait(seconds)
                    end,
                    log = log,
                }
            end

            return table.freeze(Executor)
        end

        function __ANTIBRAIN_MODULES.av()

            if not v then
                v = {
                    c = __modImpl(),

    do
        local function __modImpl()
            local Failure = __ANTIBRAIN_MODULES.a()
            local VERSION = 1
            local Options = {}

            function Options.new()
                return {options = {}}
            end
            function Options.add(registry, option)
                assert(not registry.options[option.id], string.format('Option id "%s" is already used', tostring(option.id)))

                registry.options[option.id] = option
            end
            function Options.save(registry, only, into)
                local saved = into or {
                    version = VERSION,
                    options = {},
                }

                for id, option in registry.options do
                    if not only or only[id] then
                        local ok, value = pcall(option.save)

                        if ok then
                            saved.options[id] = value
                        end
                    end
                end

                return saved
            end
            function Options.load(registry, saved, only)
                if type(saved) ~= 'table' or type(saved.options) ~= 'table' then
                    return 'it is not a saved config'
                end
                if saved.version ~= VERSION then
                    return string.format('it was saved in layout %s, which this version cannot read', tostring(saved.version))
                end

                local failed = {}

                for id, value in saved.options do
                    local option = (type(id) == 'string' and {
                        registry.options[id],
                    } or {nil})[1]

                    if option and (not only or only[id]) then
                        local failure = Failure.capture(option.load, value)

                        if failure then
                            table.insert(failed, id)
                        end
                    end
                end

                if #failed > 0 then
                    table.sort(failed)

                    return string.format('these values could not be applied: %s', tostring(table.concat(failed, ', ')))
                end

                return nil
            end

            return table.freeze(Options)
        end

        function __ANTIBRAIN_MODULES.aw()

            end

    do
        local function __modImpl()
            local Failure = __ANTIBRAIN_MODULES.a()
            local Signal = {}

            function Signal.new(report)
                local entries = {}
                local signal = {
                    connect = function(_, listener)
                        local entry = {
                            listener = listener,
                            connected = true,
                        }

                        table.insert(entries, entry)

                        return function()
                            entry.connected = false

                            local at = table.find(entries, entry)

                            if at then
                                table.remove(entries, at)
                            end
                        end
                    end,
                    fire = function(_, ...)
                        for _, entry in table.clone(entries)do
                            if entry.connected then
                                local failure = Failure.capture(entry.listener, 
...)

                                if failure then
                                    pcall(report, failure)
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end,
                }

                return signal
            end

            return table.freeze(Signal)
        end

        function __ANTIBRAIN_MODULES.ax()
            local v = __ANTIBRAIN_MODULES.cache.ax

            if not v then
                v = {
                    c = __modImpl(),
                }
                __ANTIBRAIN_MODULES.cache.ax = v
            end

            return v.c
        end
    end
    do
        local function __modImpl()
            __ANTIBRAIN_MODULES.b()

            local Options = __ANTIBRAIN_MODULES.aw()
            local Signal = __ANTIBRAIN_MODULES.ax()
            local Data = {}

            function Data.new(registry, id, default, report)
                local changed = Signal.new(report)
                local data = {
                    id = id,
                    value = default,
                    set = function(self, value)
                        if value == self.value then
                            return
                        end

                        self.value = value

                        changed:fire(value)
                    end,
                    onChanged = function(_, callback)
                        return changed:connect(callback)
                    end,
                }

                Options.add(registry, {
                    id = id,
                    save = function()
                        return data.value
                    end,
                    load = function(saved)
                        if type(saved) == 'string' then
                            data:set(saved)
                        end
                    end,
                })

                return data
            end

            return table.freeze(Data)
        end

    do
        local function __modImpl()
            local Scope = __ANTIBRAIN_MODULES.c()
            local Services = __ANTIBRAIN_MODULES.k()
            local Backdrop = {}
            local apply

            function Backdrop.new(scope, gui, mobile)
                local dim = Scope.own(scope, Instance.new('Frame'))

                dim.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0)
                dim.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                dim.BorderSizePixel = 0
                dim.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
                dim.ZIndex = 0
                dim.Visible = false
                dim.Parent = gui

                local backdrop = {
                    scope = scope,
                    settings = {
                        enabled = true,
                        color = Color3.new(0, 0, 0),
                        transparency = 0.7,
                        blur = mobile and 0 or 15,
                        contrast = 0,
                        saturation = 0,
                        brightness = 0,
                    },
                    open = false,
                    dim = dim,
                    blur = nil,
                    correction = nil,
                }

                scope:add(function()
                    backdrop.open = false
                    backdrop.dim.Visible = false
                end)
                scope:add(function()
                    local blur = backdrop.blur

                    backdrop.blur = nil

                    if blur then
                        blur:Destroy()
                    end
                end)
                scope:add(function()
                    local correction = backdrop.correction

                    backdrop.correction = nil

                    if correction then
                        correction:Destroy()
                    end
                end)
                scope:connect(Services.Workspace:GetPropertyChangedSignal('CurrentCamera'), function(
                )
                    apply(backdrop)
                end)

                return backdrop
            end

            local function kept(current, class)
                if current then
                    if current.Parent then
                        return current
                    end

                    current:Destroy()
                end

                local made = Instance.new(class)
                local ok, failure = pcall(function()
                    made.Name = '\0'
                end)

                if not ok then
                    made:Destroy()
                    error(failure, 0)
                end

                return made
            end

            apply = function(backdrop)
                local settings = backdrop.settings
                local on = backdrop.open and settings.enabled
                local camera = Services.Workspace.CurrentCamera or Services.Lighting

                if on and (settings.contrast ~= 0 or settings.saturation ~= 0 or settings.brightness ~= 0) then
                    local correction = (kept(backdrop.correction, 'ColorCorrectionEffect'))

                    backdrop.correction = correction
                    correction.Contrast = settings.contrast
                    correction.Saturation = settings.saturation
                    correction.Brightness = settings.brightness
                    correction.Parent = camera
                elseif backdrop.correction then
                    backdrop.correction:Destroy()

                    backdrop.correction = nil
                end
                if on and settings.blur > 0 then
                    local blur = (kept(backdrop.blur, 'BlurEffect'))

                    backdrop.blur = blur
                    blur.Size = settings.blur
                    blur.Parent = camera
                elseif backdrop.blur then
                    backdrop.blur:Destroy()

                    backdrop.blur = nil
                end

                local dim = backdrop.dim

                dim.BackgroundColor3 = settings.color
                dim.BackgroundTransparency = (on and {
                    settings.transparency,
                } or {1})[1]
                dim.Visible = on
            end

            function Backdrop.show(backdrop, open)
                if backdrop.scope.closed then
                    return
                end

                backdrop.open = open

                apply(backdrop)
            end
            function Backdrop.restyle(backdrop, change)
                if backdrop.scope.closed then
                    return
                end

                change(backdrop.settings)

                if backdrop.scope.closed then
                    return
                end

                apply(backdrop)
            end

            return table.freeze(Backdrop)
        end

    do
        local function __modImpl()
            local Palette = {}

            local function theme(
                name,
                font,
                main,
                accent,
                background,
                outline,
                risk
            )
                return table.freeze({
                    name = name,
                    colors = table.freeze({
                        font = font,
                        main = main,
                        accent = accent,
                        background = background,
                        outline = outline,
                        risk = risk,
                    }),
                })
            end

            Palette.DEFAULT = 'Default'
            Palette.BUILT_IN = table.freeze({
                theme('Default', 'ffffff', '181818', '4777b6', '141414', '1f1f1f', 'ff0000'),
                theme('Dark', 'ffffff', '0a0a0a', '5945ff', '0a0a0a', '171717', 'e50000'),
                theme('Fatality', 'ffffff', '181537', 'ca0756', '201c46', '39335a', 'e50000'),
                theme('Imgui', 'ffffff', '151617', '406ba8', '151617', '242424', 'e50000'),
                theme('Iniuria', 'ffffff', '1e1d1e', 'c41275', '1e1d1e', '33252b', 'e50000'),
                theme('Monolith', 'ffffff', '141115', 'c707bd', '171417', '272427', 'e50000'),
                theme('Neverlose', 'ffffff', '0c1014', '01a3f1', '0c0f14', '191919', 'e50000'),
                theme('Nord', 'ffffff', '1c1e20', '9effc8', '1c1e20', '24282d', 'ff7a00'),
                theme('Onetap', 'ffffff', '1e1d22', 'faa614', '18181c', '313033', 'e50000'),
                theme('Primordial', 'ffffff', '181818', 'd7a6b0', '1f1f1f', '2a2a2a', 'e50000'),
                theme('Skeet', 'ffffff', '131313', '81ff54', '151515', '2a2a2a', 'e50000'),
                theme('Tokyo Night', 'ffffff', '191925', '6956cb', '15151e', '272727', 'fb5f5f'),
                theme('V3rmillion', 'ffffff', '202020', 'cd1818', '202020', '2a2a2a', 'e50000'),
            })

            local KEYS = {
                {
                    key = 'main',
                    label = 'Main Color',
                },
                {
                    key = 'accent',
                    label = 'Accent Color',
                },
                {
                    key = 'background',
                    label = 'Background Color',
                },
                {
                    key = 'outline',
                    label = 'Outline Color',
                },
                {
                    key = 'font',
                    label = 'Text Color',
                },
                {
                    key = 'risk',
                    label = 'Risk Text Color',
                },
            }

            Palette.KEYS = table.freeze(KEYS)

            function Palette.builtIn(name)
                for _, builtIn in Palette.BUILT_IN do
                    if builtIn.name == name then
                        return builtIn
                    end
                end

                return nil
            end
            function Palette.isHex(text)
                return text:match('^#?%x%x%x%x%x%x$') ~= nil
            end
            function Palette.colors(hex)
                return {
                    font = Color3.fromHex(hex.font),
                    main = Color3.fromHex(hex.main),
                    background = Color3.fromHex(hex.background),
                    accent = Color3.fromHex(hex.accent),
                    outline = Color3.fromHex(hex.outline),
                    risk = Color3.fromHex(hex.risk),
                }
            end
            function Palette.darker(color)
                local hue, saturation, value = color:ToHSV()

                return Color3.fromHSV(hue, saturation, value / 1.5)
            end

            return table.freeze(Palette)
        end

        function __ANTIBRAIN_MODULES.aA()
